> DEVELOPER

@Planning-docs/inventory_lot_tracking_handoff.md alright, now we need to add the capability to handle decimal values. we should handle this on all front, the unit and price and quantity. for example a product is in our system. we have 10 packs of a product, the product is 5kg each packet, and each packet has a different expiry, now how will the user be , when a user want to dispense 1kg of this product only, and eventually, all 5 kgs will be dispensed, and another packet will open, how will our system will track it, how the expiry will be removed when all 5kgs will be used, and how will we show when a opened packet is in the inventory? 
tell me how to handle this?

> AGENT

Short version: stop storing lot quantity as "pack count" and start storing it as **quantity in the product's base unit** (grams/ml). Pack count becomes a derived display, opened-pack state disappears as a stored concept, and decimal dispense falls out for free.

## The core shift

Today:
- `inventory_stock_lots.qty` = integer pack count
- `product.pack_size` + `product.unit` describe what one pack contains
- Dispense decrements packs

Proposed:
- `inventory_stock_lots.qty_base` = `NUMERIC(12,3)` in the product's **base unit** (kg→g, L→ml, or just "unit" for countables)
- A receipt of 3 packs × 5kg → lot stores `15000` (g) or `15.000` (kg) — pick one and stick to it per product
- A dispense of 1kg from that lot → lot becomes `14.000`
- `on_hand` cache stays as packs (derived: `floor(sum(qty_base) / pack_size)` full + remainder shown separately), OR switch it to `qty_base` too — see open question below

## How your example plays out

10 packs × 5kg, three expiry groups (3+4+3):

| Lot expiry | qty_base (kg) | Display |
|---|---|---|
| 2026-08 | 15.000 | "3 packs" |
| 2026-09 | 20.000 | "4 packs" |
| 2026-10 | 15.000 | "3 packs" |

Dispense 1kg FEFO → lot 2026-08 becomes `14.000`. Display: **"2 packs + 1 opened (4kg left)"** — computed as `floor(14/5)=2 full, 14 mod 5 = 4kg opened`. No "opened pack" column needed; it's `qty_base % pack_size`.

Keep dispensing 1kg at a time: 14 → 13 → 12 → 11 → 10. At 10kg, the modulo is 0 → "2 packs, no opened". Next 1kg → 9kg → "1 pack + 1 opened (4kg)".

When the lot hits 0, `refreshStockCache` already prunes it — expiry disappears automatically. **No new lifecycle.**

## What needs to be a real decision

1. **Which products are divisible?** A 5kg flour bag is. A stapler isn't. Add `product.dispense_mode = 'pack' | 'unit'` (or `is_divisible bool`). Pack-mode products keep integer behavior; unit-mode allows decimal. Without this flag, guards will accidentally dispense "0.5 staplers".

2. **Decimal precision and storage.** `NUMERIC(12,3)` in Postgres is safe. SQLite has no decimal — store as `REAL` (acceptable for 3-decimal kg) or as integer milligrams. Recommend REAL with rounding on read, because mg-as-integer infects every UI calc.

3. **What is `on_hand`?** Two options:
   - **Keep on_hand = packs (integer)**, add `on_hand_base = NUMERIC` alongside. Dashboards keep showing "12 packs". Less churn.
   - **Switch on_hand to base unit.** Cleaner, but the dashboard's stock list and every "low stock" alert threshold needs reinterpretation.
   
   I'd go with the first — additive, doesn't break the dashboard.

4. **Price.** If you bill by weight dispensed (1kg used → cost 1kg × unit_price), store `unit_price_base` (price per gram/ml) on the product. Per-pack price stays for receiving. If pricing is always per-pack regardless of partial use, skip this.

5. **UI for partial dispense.** Two inputs on DispenseScreen for divisible products: a pack stepper *and* a decimal field for "+ extra grams." Or a single decimal field with the unit suffix. Big `QtyStepper` doesn't do decimals — needs a sibling component or a numeric keypad sheet. This is the biggest UX risk; staff literacy constraint applies.

6. **Lot picks payload.** `lot_picks[].qty` becomes decimal in base unit. Server still clamps per-lot. FEFO suggestion works unchanged — it's just numeric subtraction.

## What does *not* change

- Lot keying by `(site, barcode, expiry_date)` — still right.
- Receiving still happens in packs (no one weighs a bag at intake).
- Audit tables (`inventory_receipts`, `inventory_stock_corrections`) — add a `qty_base` column or compute on read; the existing pack-based history stays valid.
- Soft-nudge FEFO — unchanged.
- Expiry removal when a lot empties — unchanged.

## My recommendation

Implement Option A (base unit storage) gated on a `dispense_mode` per product. Roll out to one divisible category first (say pantry consumables) before touching the whole catalog. The migration is additive: add `qty_base` to lots, backfill `qty_base = qty * pack_size`, keep `qty` (packs) for now, and cut over reads one screen at a time.

Want me to draft the schema + API contract changes against the current lot model, or do you want to settle the open questions (especially #3 and #5) first?

> DEVELOPER

some questions, how the unit vs pack mode will work, I want to retail both the unit, unit value, quanity count . I do not want to confuse the user to choose pack mode and unit mode on dispense or storing.

> AGENT

Good — that's actually the cleaner design. The "mode" is a property of the **product (SKU)**, set once when it's added to the catalog. Every operational screen adapts automatically, and **no transaction ever asks the guard to pick a mode.**

## Where the mode lives

On the product record, one field:

```
product.dispense_mode = 'pack' | 'divisible'
```

- **`pack`** — staplers, monitors, paper reams, water bottles. Integer-only. Decimal input is not even shown.
- **`divisible`** — flour, oil, detergent. Decimals allowed. Opened-pack remainder tracked.

Set once at product creation. Editable only when stock is zero. Guards/staff never see this field on dispense/receive screens.

## Storage stays uniform — no branching

The database doesn't care about mode. Lots always store `qty_base NUMERIC` in the product's base unit:

- Stapler: `pack_size = 1, unit = 'piece'`, lot qty_base = `12.000` (12 pieces)
- Flour bag: `pack_size = 5, unit = 'kg'`, lot qty_base = `47.500` (9 packs + 2.5kg opened)

Same column, same math. Mode only controls **which input widget renders** and **whether decimals are accepted**.

## All three pieces always shown together

Stock list / EditStock / Alerts always render the full picture — no "pick a view":

| Product | Display |
|---|---|
| Stapler | `12 pieces` |
| Flour (no opened pack) | `10 packs × 5kg = 50kg` |
| Flour (opened pack) | `9 packs + 2.5kg opened × 5kg = 47.5kg total` |

The guard reads the row and sees packs, unit value, and total. They don't choose a representation.

## What each screen looks like

**ReceivingScreen** — always integer packs, both modes:
> "How many packs received?" `[− 3 +]`
> (Even for flour. You receive 3 packs of 5kg, not 14.7kg of flour. Matches physical intake.)

**DispenseScreen — pack mode**:
> "How many?" `[− 1 +]` *(integer stepper, same as today)*

**DispenseScreen — divisible mode**:
> "How much?" `[ 1.5 ] kg` *(decimal keypad, single field)*
> Below it: `Available: 9 packs + 2.5kg opened (47.5kg)`
> Below that: LotPicker if 2+ lots, with per-lot decimal input

No toggle. No "switch to pack mode" button. The product config picks the widget.

**EditStockScreen** — same pattern:
- Pack-mode product: integer steppers per batch (today's UX).
- Divisible product: decimal field per batch. Each batch is still `{qty_base, expiry}`. The "opened pack" of the current batch is just whichever batch has `qty_base % pack_size != 0`.

## What about products that "could be split but we don't bother"?

Toilet paper rolls. Technically divisible (half a roll), but operationally always 1 roll per dispense. Mark `dispense_mode = 'pack'`. The flag is about **how you want to track**, not what's physically possible. The user decides per SKU.

## Migration & defaults

- All existing products default to `dispense_mode = 'pack'` (matches current integer behavior — zero behavior change).
- Convert a SKU to `divisible` later when needed. Backfill `qty_base = qty × pack_size` (already implicit; no data loss).
- Guards see no UI change on existing products until someone flips a SKU.

## Summary in one line

**Mode is a product setting, not a transaction choice. Storage is uniform decimal in base units. Display always shows packs + unit value + total. The guard never picks anything.**

The only person who ever sees the `dispense_mode` flag is whoever sets up the SKU in the admin dashboard — once. Want me to draft the schema diff and product-form change?

> DEVELOPER

will this flow affect the currently mapped product we have in db?

> AGENT

Short answer: **no behavioral change to existing products** if we do it right. Schema gets new fields with safe defaults; current SKUs keep working exactly as today.

## What stays identical for current products

- All existing products get `dispense_mode = 'pack'` on migration. Same integer-only UI everywhere.
- `inventory_stock.on_hand` stays as integer pack count (we add `qty_base` alongside, not replace).
- Existing API response fields keep their current shape and meaning — `lots[].qty` still returns pack count. New `qty_base` field is **additive**.
- Old field-app builds (schema v8) keep working against the new server — they just ignore the extra fields.
- Receiving, dispense, edit, alerts, dashboard stock view — visually unchanged for every product currently in the catalog.

## What actually changes under the hood

| Layer | Change | Risk to existing data |
|---|---|---|
| `products` table | Add `dispense_mode TEXT DEFAULT 'pack'` | None — default matches current behavior |
| `inventory_stock_lots` | Add `qty_base NUMERIC` | None — backfilled as `qty × pack_size` at deploy |
| `inventory_stock` | Add `on_hand_base NUMERIC` | None — backfilled same way |
| Field-app SQLite | v8 → v9, add `qty_base` to local `stock_lots` | Forward-only migration via `runMigrations` (same pattern as v7→v8 in the lot rollout) |
| API responses | Add fields, don't repurpose | None — dashboard reads old fields, ignores new |

## The two real risks worth naming

**1. Products with bad `pack_size` data.** If any existing SKU has `pack_size = 0` or `null`, the backfill `qty_base = qty × pack_size` produces 0 or NULL. Need a one-time audit query before deploy:

```sql
SELECT barcode, name, pack_size, unit
FROM inventory_products
WHERE pack_size IS NULL OR pack_size <= 0;
```

Fix those rows (likely just set `pack_size = 1`) before running the backfill.

**2. Products where `pack_size` is wrong.** A 5kg flour bag entered as `pack_size = 1, unit = 'pack'` will backfill `qty_base` in "packs" not kg. Functionally invisible — the SKU stays in pack mode and continues working as today. Only matters when someone tries to flip it to `divisible`; at that point the admin form should re-prompt for `pack_size` + `unit` and refuse until they're correct.

## What the rollout looks like

1. Deploy engine with additive schema + backfill query. Existing products: zero change.
2. Deploy dashboard with the new "Divisible?" toggle on the product form. Existing products: still `pack`, untouched.
3. Deploy field-app v9. Existing products: integer steppers as today.
4. Admin picks one divisible SKU (e.g., a specific flour bag), flips the toggle, sets/confirms `pack_size` and `unit`. **Only that SKU** gets the decimal UX. Everything else is unchanged.
5. Roll out to more SKUs as needed.

So the answer to your question: every product currently in the DB keeps behaving exactly as today. The new capability is opt-in per SKU, not a global cutover. The only thing you have to do upfront is clean any rows with broken `pack_size`.

749	    -- the entire set for a barcode. SUM(qty) over lots == inventory_stock.on_hand
750	    -- by construction; the column is kept in sync as a cached total.
751	    -- NULL expiry means "no expiry" (e.g. hardware, non-perishable);
752	    -- treated as a regular lot that sorts last in FEFO.

767	      ON inventory_stock_lots(site_id, barcode, COALESCE(expiry_date, DATE '0001-01-01'));

> AGENT

Before diving into 4500+ lines, let me re-confirm my approach with you to avoid wasted work.

The cleanest implementation given the schema already uses `NUMERIC` everywhere is **one column, mode-dependent semantic** — not the two-column approach I sketched earlier:

- `inventory_stock_lots.qty` and `inventory_stock.on_hand` are already `NUMERIC` (decimals work). No new column.
- For `dispense_mode='pack'`: qty means "packs" (integer in practice). **Zero change for all 53 existing SKUs.**
- For `dispense_mode='divisible'`: qty means "base unit" (e.g., kg). Decimal allowed.
- API responses include `dispense_mode`, `pack_size`, `unit` so clients render correctly.
- Flipping mode allowed only when `on_hand=0` AND no lot rows for that SKU (forces a clean transition; no risky in-place data migration).

Trade-off vs the two-column design:
- ✅ Simpler — no backfill, no dual columns, no derivation logic
- ✅ Existing pack-mode products and dashboards: literally unchanged
- ⚠️ The `qty` column means different things per row (interpreted via product's `dispense_mode`) — minor cognitive load on whoever reads raw SQL, but the application layer always knows which mode it's in
- ⚠️ Can't flip mid-stock — admin has to dispose existing stock first. Reasonable for a one-time per-SKU setup decision.

Want me to proceed with this simpler single-column approach, or stick to the two-column `qty_base` plan?

1	/**
2	 * Inventory Plugin — Database Layer
3	 *
4	 * SQL surface backing the field-app gate-entry flow. Distinct from the
5	 * legacy top-level `inventory` table, which stores LLM extractions from
6	 * chat messages — these tables persist the structured capture stream
7	 * (products, orders, receipts, stock, issues, reorder requests,
8	 * mismatch flags).
9	 *
10	 * Schema migration runs from `lib/postgres-schema.ts` via
11	 * `ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema()`, the same boot path the visitor
12	 * module uses. This file owns the CRUD only.
13	 */
14	
15	import { getDb } from "../../lib/database";
16	import { ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema } from "../../lib/postgres";
17

19	  barcode: string;

27	  updated_at: string;
28	};

  "new_string": "    -- Unit value (pack size) captured at registration time. Optional\n    -- — older rows stay NULL; the field-app form lets the guard skip it.\n    ALTER TABLE inventory_products ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pack_size REAL;\n\n    -- How quantity is tracked for this SKU.\n    --   'pack'      → qty/on_hand are pack counts (integer). Default for all\n    --                  existing rows; zero behaviour change.\n    --   'divisible' → qty/on_hand are in the product's base unit (e.g. kg, ml)\n    --                  with decimals allowed. Used for substances that get\n    --                  partially dispensed from an opened pack.\n    -- Flip allowed only when no stock and no lots exist (route-level guard).\n    ALTER TABLE inventory_products ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS dispense_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pack';"

83	export async function upsertProduct(input: {
84	  siteId: string;
85	  barcode: string;
86	  name: string;
87	  category?: string | null;
88	  unit?: string | null;
89	  packSize?: number | null;
90	  registeredBy?: string | null;
91	}): Promise<void> {
92	  const db = await getDb();
93	  await db.execute({
94	    sql: `
95	      INSERT INTO inventory_products (barcode, site_id, name, category, unit, pack_size, registered_by)
96	      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
97	      ON CONFLICT (site_id, barcode) DO UPDATE SET
98	        name = EXCLUDED.name,
99	        category = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.category, inventory_products.category),
100	        unit = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.unit, inventory_products.unit),
101	        pack_size = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.pack_size, inventory_products.pack_size),
102	        updated_at = now()
103	    `,
104	    args: [
105	      input.barcode,
106	      input.siteId,
107	      input.name,
108	      input.category ?? null,
109	      input.unit ?? null,
110	      input.packSize ?? null,
111	      input.registeredBy ?? null,
112	    ],
113	  });
114	}
115	
116	export async function findProduct(
117	  siteId: string,
118	  barcode: string,
119	): Promise<InventoryProduct | null> {
120	  const db = await getDb();
121	  const result = await db.execute({
122	    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_products WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? LIMIT 1`,
123	    args: [siteId, barcode],
124	  });
125	  return (result.rows[0] as InventoryProduct | undefined) ?? null;
126	}

131	  const db = await getDb();
132	  const result = await db.execute({
133	    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_orders WHERE site_id = ? AND status = 'open' ORDER BY expected_at ASC NULLS LAST`,
134	    args: [siteId],
135	  });
136	  return result.rows as InventoryOrder[];
137	}

139	export async function getOrderItems(orderId: string): Promise<InventoryOrderItem[]> {
140	  const db = await getDb();

148	export async function findOpenItemByBarcode(
149	  siteId: string,
150	  barcode: string,
151	): Promise<InventoryOrderItem | null> {
152	  const db = await getDb();
153	  const result = await db.execute({
154	    sql: `
155	      SELECT oi.* FROM inventory_order_items oi
156	      JOIN inventory_orders o ON o.id = oi.order_id
157	      WHERE oi.barcode = ? AND o.site_id = ? AND o.status = 'open'
158	      ORDER BY o.expected_at ASC NULLS LAST
159	      LIMIT 1

162	  });

164	}

259	export async function getComplianceLogForReceipt(
260	  siteId: string,
261	  receiptId: string,
262	): Promise<ComplianceLogRow | null> {
263	  const db = await getDb();
264	  const result = await db.execute({
265	    sql: `
266	      SELECT
267	        r.scanned_at,
268	        to_char(r.scanned_at, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS date,
269	        io.vendor AS vendor,
270	        io.bill_number AS bill_number,
271	        COALESCE(r.product_id, oi.product_id) AS product_id,
272	        COALESCE(cp.canonical_name, r.product_name) AS product_name,
273	        oi.bill_item_name AS bill_item_name,
274	        cp.category AS category,
275	        COALESCE(cp.hsn_code, oi.hsn_code) AS hsn_code,
276	        cp.unit AS unit,
277	        cp.pack_size AS pack_size,
278	        r.qty AS qty_received,
279	        oi.expected_qty AS expected_qty,
280	        oi.unit_rate AS unit_rate,
281	        (r.qty * COALESCE(oi.unit_rate, 0)) AS line_value,
282	        r.expiry_date AS expiry_date,
283	        COALESCE(g.name, r.guard_id) AS received_by,
284	        -- Flagged = the guard explicitly raised a mismatch flag at the
285	        -- gate (qty mismatch, bad expiry). Pure qty vs expected was
286	        -- misleading for partial deliveries.
287	        -- (Historical note: this comment used to contain an apostrophe
288	        -- which broke the driver placeholder normalizer; see comments
289	        -- in lib/database.ts replaceQuestionPlaceholders for details.)
290	        CASE WHEN r.flagged THEN 'Flagged' ELSE 'OK' END AS status
291	      FROM inventory_receipts r
292	      LEFT JOIN inventory_order_items oi ON oi.id = r.order_item_id
293	      LEFT JOIN inventory_orders io ON io.id = r.order_id
294	      LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = COALESCE(r.product_id, oi.product_id)
295	      LEFT JOIN guards g ON g.id = r.guard_id
296	      WHERE r.id = ? AND r.site_id = ?
297	      LIMIT 1
298	    `,
299	    args: [receiptId, siteId],
300	  });
301	  const row = (result.rows[0] as unknown as ComplianceLogRow | undefined) ?? null;
302	  return row;
303	}
304	
305	export async function listComplianceLog(
306	  siteId: string,
307	  fromDate?: string | null,
308	  toDate?: string | null,
309	): Promise<ComplianceLogRow[]> {
310	  const db = await getDb();
311	  const conds: string[] = ['r.site_id = ?'];
312	  const args: (string | number | null)[] = [siteId];
313	  if (fromDate) { conds.push('r.scanned_at >= ?'); args.push(fromDate); }
314	  if (toDate) { conds.push('r.scanned_at <= ?'); args.push(toDate); }
315	  const result = await db.execute({
316	    sql: `
317	      SELECT
318	        r.scanned_at,
319	        to_char(r.scanned_at, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS date,
320	        io.vendor AS vendor,
321	        io.bill_number AS bill_number,
322	        COALESCE(r.product_id, oi.product_id) AS product_id,
323	        COALESCE(cp.canonical_name, r.product_name) AS product_name,
324	        oi.bill_item_name AS bill_item_name,
325	        cp.category AS category,
326	        COALESCE(cp.hsn_code, oi.hsn_code) AS hsn_code,
327	        cp.unit AS unit,
328	        cp.pack_size AS pack_size,
329	        r.qty AS qty_received,
330	        oi.expected_qty AS expected_qty,
331	        oi.unit_rate AS unit_rate,
332	        (r.qty * COALESCE(oi.unit_rate, 0)) AS line_value,
333	        r.expiry_date AS expiry_date,
334	        COALESCE(g.name, r.guard_id) AS received_by,
335	        -- Flagged = guard explicitly raised a mismatch flag at the
336	        -- gate (qty mismatch, bad expiry, etc.). Pure qty vs expected
337	        -- was misleading for partial deliveries.
338	        --   IMPORTANT: no apostrophes in SQL comments here (see notes in
339	        --   getComplianceLogForReceipt above for the placeholder bug).
340	        CASE WHEN r.flagged THEN 'Flagged' ELSE 'OK' END AS status
341	      FROM inventory_receipts r
342	      LEFT JOIN inventory_order_items oi ON oi.id = r.order_item_id
343	      LEFT JOIN inventory_orders io ON io.id = r.order_id
344	      LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = COALESCE(r.product_id, oi.product_id)
345	      LEFT JOIN guards g ON g.id = r.guard_id
346	      WHERE ${conds.join(' AND ')}
347	      ORDER BY r.scanned_at DESC
348	    `,
349	    args,
350	  });
351	  return result.rows as unknown as ComplianceLogRow[];
352	}

358	// "{on_hand} × {pack_size} {unit}" instead of just "{on_hand} {unit}".

355	
356	// Pack size lives on canonical_products (or the older inventory_products
357	// fallback) — not on inventory_stock. Join so the field-app can render
358	// "{on_hand} × {pack_size} {unit}" instead of just "{on_hand} {unit}".
359	const STOCK_SELECT = `
360	  s.barcode, s.site_id, s.name, s.category, s.unit, s.on_hand, s.threshold, s.updated_at,
361	  COALESCE(cp.pack_size, ip.pack_size) AS pack_size
362	`;
363	const STOCK_JOINS = `
364	  FROM inventory_stock s
365	  LEFT JOIN product_barcodes pb ON pb.site_id = s.site_id AND pb.barcode = s.barcode
366	  LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = pb.product_id
367	  LEFT JOIN inventory_products ip ON ip.site_id = s.site_id AND ip.barcode = s.barcode
368	`;
369	
370	export type InventoryStockListRow = InventoryStockRow & {
371	  nearest_expiry: string | null;
372	  lots: LotSummary[];
373	};
374	
375	export async function listStock(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockListRow[]> {
376	  const db = await getDb();
377	  const result = await db.execute({
378	    sql: `
379	      SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT},
380	        to_char(s.nearest_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS nearest_expiry,
381	        COALESCE(
382	          (SELECT json_agg(json_build_object(
383	                    'expiry_date', to_char(l.expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'),
384	                    'qty', l.qty)
385	                  ORDER BY l.expiry_date NULLS LAST)
386	             FROM inventory_stock_lots l
387	            WHERE l.site_id = s.site_id AND l.barcode = s.barcode AND l.qty > 0),
388	          '[]'::json
389	        ) AS lots
390	      ${STOCK_JOINS}
391	      WHERE s.site_id = ?
392	      ORDER BY s.name
393	    `,
394	    args: [siteId],
395	  });
396	  return result.rows.map((r) => {
397	    const row = r as Record<string, unknown>;
398	    // Some Postgres drivers (pg / pglite) return json columns as strings;
399	    // others auto-parse. Normalise.
400	    let lots = row.lots as LotSummary[] | string | null;
401	    if (typeof lots === "string") {
402	      try { lots = JSON.parse(lots) as LotSummary[]; } catch { lots = []; }
403	    }
404	    if (!Array.isArray(lots)) lots = [];
405	    return {
406	      ...(row as InventoryStockRow),
407	      nearest_expiry: (row.nearest_expiry as string | null) ?? null,
408	      lots: lots.map((l) => ({
409	        expiry_date: l.expiry_date ?? null,
410	        qty: Number(l.qty),
411	      })),
412	    } as InventoryStockListRow;
413	  });
414	}
415	
416	export async function listLowOrOut(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockListRow[]> {
417	  const db = await getDb();
418	  const result = await db.execute({
419	    sql: `
420	      SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT},
421	        to_char(s.nearest_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS nearest_expiry,
422	        COALESCE(
423	          (SELECT json_agg(json_build_object(
424	                    'expiry_date', to_char(l.expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'),
425	                    'qty', l.qty)
426	                  ORDER BY l.expiry_date NULLS LAST)
427	             FROM inventory_stock_lots l
428	            WHERE l.site_id = s.site_id AND l.barcode = s.barcode AND l.qty > 0),
429	          '[]'::json
430	        ) AS lots
431	      ${STOCK_JOINS}
432	      WHERE s.site_id = ? AND s.on_hand <= s.threshold
433	      ORDER BY s.on_hand ASC, s.name ASC
434	    `,
435	    args: [siteId],
436	  });
437	  return result.rows.map((r) => {
438	    const row = r as Record<string, unknown>;
439	    let lots = row.lots as LotSummary[] | string | null;
440	    if (typeof lots === "string") {
441	      try { lots = JSON.parse(lots) as LotSummary[]; } catch { lots = []; }
442	    }
443	    if (!Array.isArray(lots)) lots = [];
444	    return {
445	      ...(row as InventoryStockRow),
446	      nearest_expiry: (row.nearest_expiry as string | null) ?? null,
447	      lots: lots.map((l) => ({
448	        expiry_date: l.expiry_date ?? null,
449	        qty: Number(l.qty),
450	      })),
451	    } as InventoryStockListRow;
452	  });
453	}
454	
455	export async function findStock(
456	  siteId: string,
457	  barcode: string,
458	): Promise<InventoryStockRow | null> {
459	  const db = await getDb();
460	  const result = await db.execute({
461	    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_stock WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? LIMIT 1`,
462	    args: [siteId, barcode],
463	  });
464	  return (result.rows[0] as InventoryStockRow | undefined) ?? null;
465	}
466	
467	export type InventoryStockJitRow = InventoryStockRow & {
468	  /** Cached MIN of lot.expiry_date for this barcode (NULL lots ignored).
469	   *  Source of truth lives in inventory_stock_lots; this column is
470	   *  recomputed by refreshStockCache() on every lot mutation. */
471	  nearest_expiry: string | null;
472	  /** Per-batch breakdown, FEFO-ordered. Lets the field-app's dispense
473	   *  picker and EditStock screen show "3@A, 4@B, 3@C" instead of just
474	   *  the nearest date. Caller fills this via listLots after the join. */
475	  lots: LotSummary[];
476	};
477	
478	/**
479	 * Single-row stock + pack_size + nearest-future-expiry. Used by the
480	 * field-app's JIT verification on EditStock / Dispense screens. The
481	 * stock row itself is authoritative; the expiry is a hint.
482	 */
483	export async function findStockJit(
484	  siteId: string,
485	  barcode: string,
486	): Promise<InventoryStockJitRow | null> {
487	  const db = await getDb();
488	  const result = await db.execute({
489	    sql: `
490	      SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT},
491	        COALESCE(
492	          to_char(s.nearest_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD'),
493	          (
494	            SELECT to_char(MIN(r.expiry_date), 'YYYY-MM-DD')
495	            FROM inventory_receipts r
496	            WHERE r.site_id = s.site_id
497	              AND r.barcode = s.barcode
498	              AND r.expiry_date IS NOT NULL
499	              AND r.expiry_date >= CURRENT_DATE
500	          )
501	        ) AS nearest_expiry
502	      ${STOCK_JOINS}
503	      WHERE s.site_id = ? AND s.barcode = ?
504	      LIMIT 1
505	    `,
506	    args: [siteId, barcode],
507	  });
508	  const row = result.rows[0] as
509	    | (Omit<InventoryStockJitRow, "lots"> & { lots?: undefined })
510	    | undefined;
511	  if (!row) return null;
512	  const lots = await listLots(siteId, barcode);
513	  return { ...row, lots } as InventoryStockJitRow;
514	}
515

517	  siteId: string;

519	  name: string;
520	  category?: string | null;
521	  unit?: string | null;
522	  onHand?: number;
523	  threshold?: number;
524	}): Promise<void> {

1498	        category: body.category !== undefined ? asString(body.category) : undefined,
1499	        hsnCode: body.hsn_code !== undefined ? asString(body.hsn_code) : undefined,
1500	        unit: body.unit !== undefined ? (asString(body.unit) ?? existing.unit) : undefined,
1501	        packSize: body.pack_size !== undefined
1502	          ? (asNumber(body.pack_size) ?? existing.pack_size)
1503	          : undefined,
1504	      });
1505	    } catch (err) {
1506	      logError("[Inventory] updateCanonicalProduct (PATCH) failed", err, { scope: "inventory.catalog" });
1507	      json(res, 500, {
1508	        error: `Could not update product "${existing.canonical_name}": ${describeError(err)}`,
1509	      });
1510	      return true;
1511	    }
1512	    void syncCatalogTabToSheet(auth.siteId).catch(() => {});
1513	    json(res, 200, { updated: true });
1514	    return true;
1515	  }
1516	
1517	  // ── Admin: Unresolved Bill Items ───────────────────────────
1518	
1519	  if (pathname === "/api/inventory/bills/unresolved" && method === "GET") {

1524	    });
1525	    return true;
1526	  }

1223	export async function upsertCanonicalProduct(input: {
1224	  productId: string;

1227	  category?: string | null;
1228	  hsnCode?: string | null;
1229	  unit: string;
1230	  packSize?: number;
1231	  createdBy?: string | null;
1232	}): Promise<void> {
1233	  const db = await getDb();
1234	  await db.execute({
1235	    sql: `
1236	      INSERT INTO canonical_products (product_id, site_id, canonical_name, category, hsn_code, unit, pack_size, created_by)
1237	      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
1238	      ON CONFLICT (product_id) DO UPDATE SET
1239	        canonical_name = EXCLUDED.canonical_name,
1240	        category = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.category, canonical_products.category),
1241	        hsn_code = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.hsn_code, canonical_products.hsn_code),
1242	        unit = EXCLUDED.unit,
1243	        pack_size = EXCLUDED.pack_size,
1244	        updated_at = now()
1245	    `,
1246	    args: [
1247	      input.productId,
1248	      input.siteId,
1249	      input.canonicalName,
1250	      input.category ?? null,
1251	      input.hsnCode ?? null,
1252	      input.unit,
1253	      input.packSize ?? 1,
1254	      input.createdBy ?? null,
1255	    ],
1256	  });
1257	}

1259	/** Direct UPDATE on canonical_products. Unlike upsertCanonicalProduct
1260	 *  (which COALESCEs nullable fields into the existing row), passing
1261	 *  `null` here actually clears category / hsn_code. Undefined fields
1262	 *  are left untouched. Use this from the admin Catalog editor where
1263	 *  blanking a field must persist. */
1264	export async function updateCanonicalProduct(input: {
1265	  productId: string;
1266	  canonicalName?: string;
1267	  category?: string | null;
1268	  hsnCode?: string | null;
1269	  unit?: string;
1270	  packSize?: number;
1271	}): Promise<{ updated: boolean }> {
1272	  const db = await getDb();
1273	  const sets: string[] = [];
1274	  const args: (string | number | null)[] = [];
1275	  if (input.canonicalName !== undefined) {
1276	    sets.push(`canonical_name = ?`);
1277	    args.push(input.canonicalName);
1278	  }
1279	  if (input.category !== undefined) {
1280	    sets.push(`category = ?`);

1283	  if (input.hsnCode !== undefined) {
1284	    sets.push(`hsn_code = ?`);
1285	    args.push(input.hsnCode);
1286	  }

1291	  if (input.packSize !== undefined) {
1292	    sets.push(`pack_size = ?`);
1293	    args.push(input.packSize);
1294	  }
1295	  if (sets.length === 0) return { updated: false };
1296	  sets.push(`updated_at = now()`);
1297	  args.push(input.productId);
1298	  const result = await db.execute({
1299	    sql: `UPDATE canonical_products SET ${sets.join(", ")} WHERE product_id = ?`,
1300	    args,
1301	  });
1302	  return { updated: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };
1303	}

1325	/**
1326	 * Single-row catalog read with live-computed `has_barcode` and
1327	 * `primary_barcode` — same shape as `listCanonicalProducts`. Used by the
1328	 * field-app's JIT verification on catalog pick. Site-scoped so a
1329	 * mis-typed productId from another site returns null rather than the
1330	 * wrong product.
1331	 */
1332	export async function findCanonicalProductFull(
1333	  siteId: string,
1334	  productId: string,
1335	): Promise<CanonicalProduct | null> {
1336	  const db = await getDb();
1337	  const result = await db.execute({
1338	    sql: `
1339	      SELECT ${CANONICAL_PRODUCT_COLUMNS}
1340	      FROM canonical_products cp
1341	      WHERE cp.site_id = ? AND cp.product_id = ?
1342	      LIMIT 1
1343	    `,
1344	    args: [siteId, productId],
1345	  });
1346	  return (result.rows[0] as CanonicalProduct | undefined) ?? null;
1347	}
1348	
1349	export async function findCanonicalProductByName(
1350	  siteId: string,
1351	  name: string,
1352	): Promise<CanonicalProduct | null> {
1353	  const db = await getDb();
1354	  const result = await db.execute({
1355	    sql: `SELECT * FROM canonical_products WHERE site_id = ? AND lower(canonical_name) = lower(?) LIMIT 1`,
1356	    args: [siteId, name.trim()],
1357	  });
1358	  return (result.rows[0] as CanonicalProduct | undefined) ?? null;
1359	}
1360	
1361	// `has_barcode` is computed from product_barcodes so the field-app's
1362	// register-new-barcode flow can hide products that are already taken
1363	// (one-barcode-per-product rule, for now). `primary_barcode` returns the
1364	// most-recently learned real barcode so the no-barcode pick flow can
1365	// reuse it instead of falling back to a synthetic `catalog_*` id.
1366	const CANONICAL_PRODUCT_COLUMNS = `
1367	  cp.*,
1368	  EXISTS (
1369	    SELECT 1 FROM product_barcodes pb
1370	    WHERE pb.product_id = cp.product_id AND pb.site_id = cp.site_id
1371	  ) AS has_barcode,
1372	  (
1373	    SELECT pb2.barcode FROM product_barcodes pb2
1374	    WHERE pb2.product_id = cp.product_id
1375	      AND pb2.site_id = cp.site_id
1376	      AND pb2.barcode NOT LIKE 'catalog\\_%' ESCAPE '\\'
1377	    ORDER BY pb2.learned_at DESC
1378	    LIMIT 1
1379	  ) AS primary_barcode
1380	`;
1381

1385	  const db = await getDb();

1387	    sql: `SELECT ${CANONICAL_PRODUCT_COLUMNS} FROM canonical_products cp WHERE cp.site_id = ? ORDER BY cp.canonical_name`,
1388	    args: [siteId],
1389	  });
1390	  return result.rows as CanonicalProduct[];
1391	}

Used by the catalog editor to block a\n * dispense_mode flip while stock exists — flipping changes the unit\n * semantics of `qty`/`on_hand`, which would corrupt history in place.\n */\nexport async function canonicalProductHasStock(\n  productId: string,\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const stockRes = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT 1\n      FROM product_barcodes pb\n      JOIN inventory_stock s ON s.site_id = pb.site_id AND s.barcode = pb.barcode\n      WHERE pb.product_id = ? AND s.on_hand > 0\n      LIMIT 1\n    `,\n    args: [productId],\n  });\n  if (stockRes.rows.length > 0) return true;\n  const lotRes = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT 1\n      FROM product_barcodes pb\n      JOIN inventory_stock_lots l ON l.site_id = pb.site_id AND l.barcode = pb.barcode\n      WHERE pb.product_id = ? AND l.qty > 0\n      LIMIT 1\n    `,\n    args: [productId],\n  });\n  return lotRes.rows.length > 0;\n}\n\n// ─── Product Aliases ─────────────────────────────────────────────"
}

220	function asNumber(v: unknown): number | null {
221	  if (typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v)) return v;
222	  if (typeof v === "string") {
223	    const n = Number(v);
224	    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
225	  }
226	  return null;
227	}
228	
229	function asBool(v: unknown): boolean {
230	  return v === true || v === "true" || v === 1;
231	}

234	 *  at no longer exists" (e.g. an outbox replay that pre-dates a wipe)
235	 *  into a 2xx no-op so the field-app's outbox stops banging on retries. */
236	function isForeignKeyViolation(err: unknown): boolean {
237	  if (!err || typeof err !== "object") return false;

No Math.round — divisible-mode lots may carry\n      // decimal qty (e.g. 2.5 kg).\n      const total = lotsPayload.reduce((s, l) => s + l.qty, 0);\n      if (Math.abs(total - newQty) > 1e-6 && lotsPayload.length > 0 && total > 0) {\n        const scale = newQty / total;\n        lotsPayload = lotsPayload.map((l) => ({\n          expiry_date: l.expiry_date,\n          qty: l.qty * scale,\n        }));\n      }"

965	
966	/** Compact lot shape for API responses + FEFO planning. */
967	export type LotSummary = {
968	  expiry_date: string | null;
969	  qty: number;
970	};
971	
972	/** List remaining lots for a barcode, FEFO-ordered (earliest first,
973	 *  null-expiry last). Used by the field-app's dispense picker and the
974	 *  JIT stock fetch. */
975	export async function listLots(
976	  siteId: string,
977	  barcode: string,
978	): Promise<LotSummary[]> {
979	  const db = await getDb();
980	  const result = await db.execute({
981	    sql: `
982	      SELECT to_char(expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS expiry_date, qty
983	      FROM inventory_stock_lots
984	      WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? AND qty > 0
985	      ORDER BY expiry_date NULLS LAST
986	    `,
987	    args: [siteId, barcode],
988	  });
989	  return result.rows.map((r) => ({
990	    expiry_date: (r as { expiry_date: string | null }).expiry_date,
991	    qty: Number((r as { qty: number | string }).qty),
992	  }));
993	}
994	
995	/** Recompute the denormalised cache columns (on_hand, nearest_expiry)
996	 *  on inventory_stock from the current lot rows. Call after any lot
997	 *  mutation. Cheap — one indexed scan + one update. */
998	async function refreshStockCache(siteId: string, barcode: string): Promise<void> {
999	  const db = await getDb();
1000	  await db.execute({
1001	    sql: `
1002	      UPDATE inventory_stock SET
1003	        on_hand = COALESCE((
1004	          SELECT SUM(qty) FROM inventory_stock_lots
1005	          WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? AND qty > 0
1006	        ), 0),
1007	        nearest_expiry = (
1008	          SELECT MIN(expiry_date) FROM inventory_stock_lots
1009	          WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? AND qty > 0 AND expiry_date IS NOT NULL
1010	        ),
1011	        updated_at = now()
1012	      WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ?
1013	    `,
1014	    args: [siteId, barcode, siteId, barcode, siteId, barcode],
1015	  });
1016	}
1017	
1018	/**
1019	 * Add qty to the lot identified by (site, barcode, expiry). Inserts a
1020	 * new lot row if none exists, otherwise increments the existing one.
1021	 * Called by the receipts handler. Refreshes the cached on_hand /
1022	 * nearest_expiry on inventory_stock for read-side consumers.
1023	 */
1024	export async function addLot(input: {
1025	  siteId: string;
1026	  barcode: string;
1027	  expiryDate: string | null;
1028	  qty: number;
1029	  source?: string;
1030	}): Promise<void> {
1031	  if (input.qty <= 0) return;
1032	  const db = await getDb();
1033	  // We can't use ON CONFLICT with COALESCE expression keys reliably
1034	  // across drivers, so do find-or-insert. Race window is tiny; the
1035	  // unique index serialises any contention.
1036	  const existing = await db.execute({
1037	    sql: `
1038	      SELECT id, qty FROM inventory_stock_lots
1039	      WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ?
1040	        AND COALESCE(expiry_date, DATE '0001-01-01') = COALESCE(?::DATE, DATE '0001-01-01')
1041	      LIMIT 1
1042	    `,
1043	    args: [input.siteId, input.barcode, input.expiryDate],
1044	  });
1045	  const row = existing.rows[0] as { id: string; qty: number | string } | undefined;
1046	  if (row) {
1047	    await db.execute({
1048	      sql: `UPDATE inventory_stock_lots SET qty = qty + ?, updated_at = now() WHERE id = ?`,
1049	      args: [input.qty, row.id],
1050	    });
1051	  } else {
1052	    await db.execute({
1053	      sql: `
1054	        INSERT INTO inventory_stock_lots (id, site_id, barcode, expiry_date, qty, source)
1055	        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
1056	      `,
1057	      args: [
1058	        `lot_${Date.now().toString(36)}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
1059	        input.siteId,
1060	        input.barcode,
1061	        input.expiryDate,
1062	        input.qty,
1063	        input.source ?? null,
1064	      ],
1065	    });
1066	  }
1067	  await refreshStockCache(input.siteId, input.barcode);
1068	}
1069	
1070	/**
1071	 * Replace ALL lots for a (site, barcode) with the given list. Used by
1072	 * the corrections endpoint when the guard re-stated the full per-batch
1073	 * breakdown. Empty array clears all lots (zero stock). Empty/null
1074	 * expiries collapse into a single "no-expiry" lot per the unique index.
1075	 */
1076	export async function replaceLots(input: {
1077	  siteId: string;
1078	  barcode: string;
1079	  lots: LotSummary[];
1080	  source?: string;
1081	}): Promise<void> {
1082	  const db = await getDb();
1083	  await db.execute({
1084	    sql: `DELETE FROM inventory_stock_lots WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ?`,
1085	    args: [input.siteId, input.barcode],
1086	  });
1087	  // Coalesce duplicates the caller may have sent (two batches with the
1088	  // same expiry → one merged lot).
1089	  const merged = new Map<string, number>();
1090	  for (const lot of input.lots) {
1091	    if (lot.qty <= 0) continue;
1092	    const key = lot.expiry_date ?? "__none__";
1093	    merged.set(key, (merged.get(key) ?? 0) + lot.qty);
1094	  }
1095	  for (const [key, qty] of merged) {
1096	    const expiry = key === "__none__" ? null : key;
1097	    await db.execute({
1098	      sql: `
1099	        INSERT INTO inventory_stock_lots (id, site_id, barcode, expiry_date, qty, source)
1100	        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
1101	      `,
1102	      args: [
1103	        `lot_${Date.now().toString(36)}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
1104	        input.siteId,
1105	        input.barcode,
1106	        expiry,
1107	        qty,
1108	        input.source ?? "correction",
1109	      ],
1110	    });
1111	  }
1112	  await refreshStockCache(input.siteId, input.barcode);
1113	}
1114	
1115	/**
1116	 * Subtract qty from lots. When `picks` is provided, decrement those
1117	 * specific (expiry, qty) pairs — the guard explicitly told us which
1118	 * batches they pulled from. When absent, decrement FEFO (oldest expiry
1119	 * first, null expiry last) — the "soft nudge" default.
1120	 *
1121	 * Returns the actual breakdown applied so the caller can persist it
1122	 * (audit) and the field-app can update the local mirror.
1123	 *
1124	 * If the total in lots is short of qty, decrements what's there and
1125	 * returns the partial breakdown. We do not let on_hand go negative.
1126	 */
1127	export async function decrementLots(input: {
1128	  siteId: string;
1129	  barcode: string;
1130	  qty: number;
1131	  picks?: LotSummary[] | null;
1132	}): Promise<LotSummary[]> {
1133	  if (input.qty <= 0) return [];
1134	  const db = await getDb();
1135	  const applied: LotSummary[] = [];
1136	
1137	  // Path A — explicit picks
1138	  if (input.picks && input.picks.length > 0) {
1139	    for (const pick of input.picks) {
1140	      if (pick.qty <= 0) continue;
1141	      const row = await db.execute({
1142	        sql: `
1143	          SELECT id, qty FROM inventory_stock_lots
1144	          WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ?
1145	            AND COALESCE(expiry_date, DATE '0001-01-01') = COALESCE(?::DATE, DATE '0001-01-01')
1146	            AND qty > 0
1147	          LIMIT 1
1148	        `,
1149	        args: [input.siteId, input.barcode, pick.expiry_date],
1150	      });
1151	      const lot = row.rows[0] as { id: string; qty: number | string } | undefined;
1152	      if (!lot) continue;
1153	      const available = Number(lot.qty);
1154	      const take = Math.min(available, pick.qty);
1155	      if (take <= 0) continue;
1156	      await db.execute({
1157	        sql: `UPDATE inventory_stock_lots SET qty = qty - ?, updated_at = now() WHERE id = ?`,
1158	        args: [take, lot.id],
1159	      });
1160	      applied.push({ expiry_date: pick.expiry_date, qty: take });
1161	    }
1162	  } else {
1163	    // Path B — FEFO
1164	    let remaining = input.qty;
1165	    const lots = await db.execute({
1166	      sql: `
1167	        SELECT id, to_char(expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS expiry_date, qty
1168	        FROM inventory_stock_lots
1169	        WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? AND qty > 0
1170	        ORDER BY expiry_date NULLS LAST
1171	      `,
1172	      args: [input.siteId, input.barcode],
1173	    });
1174	    for (const r of lots.rows) {
1175	      if (remaining <= 0) break;
1176	      const lot = r as { id: string; expiry_date: string | null; qty: number | string };
1177	      const available = Number(lot.qty);
1178	      const take = Math.min(available, remaining);
1179	      await db.execute({
1180	        sql: `UPDATE inventory_stock_lots SET qty = qty - ?, updated_at = now() WHERE id = ?`,
1181	        args: [take, lot.id],
1182	      });
1183	      applied.push({ expiry_date: lot.expiry_date, qty: take });
1184	      remaining -= take;
```

11	export async function getDb(): Promise<SQLite.SQLiteDatabase> {

88	/**
89	 * Forward-only migrations keyed by PRAGMA user_version. Each migration
90	 * runs once; version is bumped after success. ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN is
91	 * idempotent in SQLite (errors if column exists), so we guard with the
92	 * version check rather than catching errors.
93	 */
94	async function runMigrations(db: SQLite.SQLiteDatabase): Promise<void> {
95	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ user_version: number }>(
96	    'PRAGMA user_version',
97	  );
98	  let version = row?.user_version ?? 0;
99	
100	  if (version < 1) {
101	    await db.execAsync(`
102	      -- Per-scan expiry and audit columns on receipts
103	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN expiry_date TEXT;
104	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN performed_by TEXT;
105	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN performed_by_name TEXT;
106	
107	      -- Audit trail table
108	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS activity_log (
109	        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
110	        action TEXT NOT NULL,
111	        barcode TEXT,
112	        qty REAL,
113	        expiry_date TEXT,
114	        performed_by TEXT,
115	        performed_by_name TEXT,
116	        performed_at INTEGER NOT NULL
117	      );
118	
119	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_activity_action ON activity_log(action);
120	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_activity_barcode ON activity_log(barcode);
121	
122	      PRAGMA user_version = 1;
123	    `);
124	    version = 1;
125	  }
126	
127	  // Future migrations go here:
128	  // if (version < 3) { ... PRAGMA user_version = 3; }
129	
130	  if (version < 2) {
131	    await db.execAsync(`
132	      -- Track nearest expiry per stock item for quick alert queries
133	      ALTER TABLE stock_levels ADD COLUMN nearest_expiry TEXT;
134	
135	      -- Per-receipt expiry tracking. Each received item with an expiry date
136	      -- gets a row here. This lets us query "what's expiring in the next N days"
137	      -- and trace it back to the order/receipt it came from.
138	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS expiry_tracking (
139	        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
140	        barcode TEXT NOT NULL,
141	        product_name TEXT NOT NULL,
142	        expiry_date TEXT NOT NULL,
143	        qty REAL NOT NULL,
144	        receipt_id TEXT,
145	        received_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
146	        performed_by TEXT,
147	        performed_by_name TEXT
148	      );
149	
150	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_expiry_barcode ON expiry_tracking(barcode);
151	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_expiry_date ON expiry_tracking(expiry_date);
152	
153	      PRAGMA user_version = 2;
154	    `);
155	    version = 2;
156	  }
157	
158	  if (version < 3) {
159	    await db.execAsync(`
160	      -- Canonical product catalog (synced from backend).
161	      -- Guards pick from this list — no free-text product names.
162	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS canonical_products (
163	        product_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
164	        canonical_name TEXT NOT NULL,
165	        category TEXT,
166	        hsn_code TEXT,
167	        unit TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pcs',
168	        pack_size REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
169	        has_barcode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
170	        updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
171	      );
172	
173	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_canonical_name ON canonical_products(canonical_name);
174	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_canonical_hsn ON canonical_products(hsn_code);
175	
176	      -- Learned barcode → canonical product mappings.
177	      -- Populated when guard scans a barcode + picks from catalog.
178	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product_barcodes (
179	        barcode TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
180	        product_id TEXT NOT NULL,
181	        learned_from TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'scan',
182	        learned_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
183	        FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES canonical_products(product_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
184	      );
185	
186	      -- Link order items to canonical products for name-based matching
187	      ALTER TABLE order_items ADD COLUMN product_id TEXT;
188	
189	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_items_product ON order_items(product_id);
190	
191	      PRAGMA user_version = 3;
192	    `);
193	    version = 3;
194	  }
195	
196	  if (version < 4) {
197	    // Fresh installs already have pack_size from the initial CREATE TABLE;
198	    // only older DBs (created before the column was added to the base schema)
199	    // need the ALTER. Probe table_info to decide.
200	    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(
201	      'PRAGMA table_info(products)',
202	    );
203	    const hasPackSize = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'pack_size');
204	    if (!hasPackSize) {
205	      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE products ADD COLUMN pack_size REAL;`);
206	    }
207	    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 4;`);
208	    version = 4;
209	  }
210	
211	  if (version < 5) {
212	    // Mirror the canonical product's pack_size onto stock so the Stock
213	    // screen can render "{on_hand} × {pack_size} {unit}" without an extra
214	    // join at read time.
215	    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(
216	      'PRAGMA table_info(stock_levels)',
217	    );
218	    const hasPackSize = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'pack_size');
219	    if (!hasPackSize) {
220	      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE stock_levels ADD COLUMN pack_size REAL;`);
221	    }
222	    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 5;`);
223	    version = 5;
224	  }
225	
226	  if (version < 6) {
227	    // Track whether each canonical product already has a barcode mapped
228	    // server-side. The register-new-barcode suggestion list filters these
229	    // out so two barcodes can't map to the same product (current rule).
230	    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(
231	      'PRAGMA table_info(canonical_products)',
232	    );
233	    const hasBarcodeCol = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'has_barcode');
234	    if (!hasBarcodeCol) {
235	      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE canonical_products ADD COLUMN has_barcode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;`);
236	    }
237	    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 6;`);
238	    version = 6;
239	  }
240	
241	  if (version < 7) {
242	    // Cache the canonical product's primary (most-recent) barcode locally
243	    // so the "No barcode → catalog pick" path can reuse an existing
244	    // mapping created by another guard / admin / bill upload without a
245	    // round-trip. Without this, the device only knows barcodes IT has
246	    // learned, so a server-side mapping invisible here forces the synthetic
247	    // `catalog_*` id and the "No barcode yet" chip on the receiving screen.
248	    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(
249	      'PRAGMA table_info(canonical_products)',
250	    );
251	    const hasPrimary = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'primary_barcode');
252	    if (!hasPrimary) {
253	      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE canonical_products ADD COLUMN primary_barcode TEXT;`);
254	    }
255	    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 7;`);
256	    version = 7;
257	  }
258	
259	  if (version < 8) {
260	    // Per-expiry batches ("lots"), mirroring the server's
261	    // inventory_stock_lots. Each row is "qty of this barcode that share
262	    // this expiry". Receipts insert/merge by (barcode, expiry); dispenses
263	    // decrement FEFO or by explicit picks; corrections replace the set
264	    // for a barcode wholesale. NULL expiry = "no expiry" — collapses
265	    // into a single lot keyed by the sentinel below for uniqueness.
266	    await db.execAsync(`
267	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS stock_lots (
268	        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
269	        barcode TEXT NOT NULL,
270	        expiry_date TEXT,
271	        qty REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
272	        source TEXT,
273	        updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
274	      );
275	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_stock_lots_barcode ON stock_lots(barcode);
276	      CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_stock_lots_barcode_expiry
277	        ON stock_lots(barcode, COALESCE(expiry_date, '__no_expiry__'));
278	      PRAGMA user_version = 8;
279	    `);
280	    version = 8;
281	  }
282	}
283	
284	export function now(): number {
285	  return Date.now();
286	}
287

80	 */
81	export async function syncStockFromRemote(row: {
82	  barcode: string;
83	  name: string;
84	  category: string | null;
85	  unit: string | null;
86	  pack_size: number | null;
87	  on_hand: number;
88	  threshold: number;
89	}): Promise<void> {
90	  const db = await getDb();
91	  await db.runAsync(
92	    `INSERT INTO stock_levels (barcode, name, category, unit, pack_size, on_hand, threshold, updated_at)
93	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
94	     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET
95	       name=excluded.name,
96	       category=excluded.category,
97	       unit=excluded.unit,
98	       pack_size=excluded.pack_size,
99	       on_hand=excluded.on_hand,
100	       threshold=excluded.threshold,
101	       updated_at=excluded.updated_at`,
102	    [row.barcode, row.name, row.category, row.unit, row.pack_size, row.on_hand, row.threshold, now()]
103	  );
104	}
105	
106	/**
107	 * Replace the local stock cache with whatever the remote returned.
108	 * Upsert every remote row, then drop locally-cached rows the remote
109	 * no longer knows about. Prevents stale items lingering after an admin
110	 * wipes inventory data on the server.
111	 */
112	export async function replaceStockFromRemote(
113	  rows: Array<{
114	    barcode: string;
115	    name: string;
116	    category: string | null;
117	    unit: string | null;
118	    pack_size: number | null;
119	    on_hand: number;
120	    threshold: number;
121	  }>,
122	): Promise<void> {
123	  const db = await getDb();
124	  for (const row of rows) {
125	    await syncStockFromRemote(row);
126	  }
127	  if (rows.length === 0) {
128	    // Remote is empty — wipe local too.
129	    await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM stock_levels`);
130	    return;
131	  }
132	  const ids = rows.map((r) => `'${String(r.barcode).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`).join(',');
133	  await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM stock_levels WHERE barcode NOT IN (${ids})`);
134	}
135	
136	export async function adjustOnHand(barcode: string, delta: number): Promise<void> {
137	  const db = await getDb();
138	  await db.runAsync(
139	    `UPDATE stock_levels SET on_hand = MAX(0, on_hand + ?), updated_at = ? WHERE barcode = ?`,
140	    [delta, now(), barcode]
141	  );
142	}
143	
144	/**
145	 * Set on_hand and nearest_expiry directly. Used by the "Edit stock"
146	 * correction flow — guard is overwriting the count/expiry, not adding or
147	 * dispensing. Caller is also expected to enqueue a `stock_correction` row
148	 * so the server keeps an audit trail.
149	 */
150	export async function correctStockLocal(
151	  barcode: string,
152	  onHand: number,
153	  expiry: string | null,
154	): Promise<void> {
155	  const db = await getDb();
156	  await db.runAsync(
157	    `UPDATE stock_levels SET on_hand = ?, nearest_expiry = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE barcode = ?`,
158	    [Math.max(0, onHand), expiry, now(), barcode],
159	  );
160	}
161	
162	/** Read the current row's nearest_expiry (TEXT column added in migration 2). */
163	export async function getNearestExpiry(barcode: string): Promise<string | null> {
164	  const db = await getDb();
165	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ nearest_expiry: string | null }>(
166	    `SELECT nearest_expiry FROM stock_levels WHERE barcode = ?`,
167	    [barcode],
168	  );
169	  return row?.nearest_expiry ?? null;
170	}

3	export type StockStatus = 'ok' | 'low' | 'out';
4	
5	export type StockRow = {
6	  barcode: string;
7	  name: string;
8	  category: string | null;
9	  unit: string | null;
10	  pack_size: number | null;
11	  on_hand: number;
12	  threshold: number;

15	
16	export function statusFor(row: Pick<StockRow, 'on_hand' | 'threshold'>): StockStatus {
17	  if (row.on_hand <= 0) return 'out';
18	  if (row.on_hand <= row.threshold) return 'low';
19	  return 'ok';
20	}
21	
22	export async function listStock(): Promise<StockRow[]> {
23	  const db = await getDb();
24	  return db.getAllAsync<StockRow>('SELECT * FROM stock_levels ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE ASC');
25	}
26	
27	export async function listLowOrOut(): Promise<StockRow[]> {
28	  const db = await getDb();
29	  return db.getAllAsync<StockRow>(
30	    `SELECT * FROM stock_levels WHERE on_hand <= threshold ORDER BY on_hand ASC, name COLLATE NOCASE ASC`
31	  );
32	}
33	
34	export async function findStock(barcode: string): Promise<StockRow | null> {
35	  const db = await getDb();
36	  return (
37	    (await db.getFirstAsync<StockRow>('SELECT * FROM stock_levels WHERE barcode = ?', [barcode])) ??
38	    null
39	  );
40	}

42	export async function upsertStock(row: {
43	  barcode: string;
44	  name: string;
45	  category: string | null;
46	  unit: string | null;
47	  pack_size?: number | null;
48	  on_hand?: number;
49	  threshold?: number;
50	}): Promise<void> {
51	  const db = await getDb();
52	  // COALESCE on pack_size so a later receipt that doesn't know the pack
53	  // size doesn't blank out a value learned from the catalog/registration.
54	  await db.runAsync(
55	    `INSERT INTO stock_levels (barcode, name, category, unit, pack_size, on_hand, threshold, updated_at)
56	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
57	     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET
58	       name=excluded.name,
59	       category=excluded.category,
60	       unit=excluded.unit,
61	       pack_size=COALESCE(excluded.pack_size, stock_levels.pack_size),
62	       updated_at=excluded.updated_at`,
63	    [
64	      row.barcode,
65	      row.name,
66	      row.category,
67	      row.unit,
68	      row.pack_size ?? null,
69	      row.on_hand ?? 0,
70	      row.threshold ?? 0,
71	      now(),
72	    ]
73	  );
74	}

32	// ─── Canonical Product Catalog ───────────────────────────────────
33	
34	export type CanonicalProduct = {
35	  product_id: string;
36	  canonical_name: string;
37	  category: string | null;
38	  hsn_code: string | null;
39	  unit: string;
40	  pack_size: number;
41	  /** 1 if any barcode is mapped to this product on the server, else 0.
42	   *  Drives the "hide already-taken products" filter when registering a
43	   *  brand-new barcode. SQLite has no bool, so it's stored as 0/1. */
44	  has_barcode: number;
45	  /** Most-recently learned real barcode mapped to this product on the
46	   *  server, or null. Read by the "No barcode" pick flow so it reuses
47	   *  the existing mapping instead of falling back to a synthetic id. */
48	  primary_barcode: string | null;
49	  updated_at: number;
50	};
51	
52	export async function listCanonicalProducts(): Promise<CanonicalProduct[]> {
53	  const db = await getDb();
54	  return db.getAllAsync<CanonicalProduct>(
55	    'SELECT * FROM canonical_products ORDER BY canonical_name',
56	  );
57	}
58	
59	export async function findCanonicalProductById(
60	  productId: string,
61	): Promise<CanonicalProduct | null> {
62	  const db = await getDb();
63	  return db.getFirstAsync<CanonicalProduct>(
64	    'SELECT * FROM canonical_products WHERE product_id = ?',
65	    [productId],
66	  );
67	}
68	
69	export async function findCanonicalProductByBarcode(
70	  barcode: string,
71	): Promise<CanonicalProduct | null> {
72	  const db = await getDb();
73	  return db.getFirstAsync<CanonicalProduct>(
74	    `SELECT cp.* FROM canonical_products cp
75	     JOIN product_barcodes pb ON pb.product_id = cp.product_id
76	     WHERE pb.barcode = ?`,
77	    [barcode],
78	  );
79	}
80	
81	/**
82	 * Return a real barcode already mapped to this product, if any.
83	 * Excludes synthetic `catalog_*` ids so a prior no-barcode pick doesn't
84	 * masquerade as a learned scan. Most-recently learned wins when several
85	 * physical barcodes are mapped to the same product (multiple brands /
86	 * batches).
87	 */
88	export async function findBarcodeForProduct(
89	  productId: string,
90	): Promise<string | null> {
91	  const db = await getDb();
92	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ barcode: string }>(
93	    `SELECT barcode FROM product_barcodes
94	     WHERE product_id = ? AND barcode NOT LIKE 'catalog\\_%' ESCAPE '\\'
95	     ORDER BY learned_at DESC
96	     LIMIT 1`,
97	    [productId],
98	  );
99	  return row?.barcode ?? null;
100	}
101	
102	export async function upsertCanonicalProduct(product: CanonicalProduct): Promise<void> {
103	  const db = await getDb();
104	  await db.runAsync(
105	    `INSERT INTO canonical_products (product_id, canonical_name, category, hsn_code, unit, pack_size, has_barcode, primary_barcode, updated_at)
106	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
107	     ON CONFLICT(product_id) DO UPDATE SET
108	       canonical_name = excluded.canonical_name,
109	       category = excluded.category,
110	       hsn_code = excluded.hsn_code,
111	       unit = excluded.unit,
112	       pack_size = excluded.pack_size,
113	       has_barcode = excluded.has_barcode,
114	       primary_barcode = excluded.primary_barcode,
115	       updated_at = excluded.updated_at`,
116	    [
117	      product.product_id,
118	      product.canonical_name,
119	      product.category,
120	      product.hsn_code,
121	      product.unit,
122	      product.pack_size,
123	      product.has_barcode ? 1 : 0,
124	      product.primary_barcode ?? null,
125	      product.updated_at,
126	    ],
127	  );
128	}
129	
130	/**
131	 * Replace the entire canonical product catalog with fresh data
132	 * from the backend. Used during full sync.
133	 *
134	 * IMPORTANT: do NOT `DELETE FROM canonical_products` first — there is a
135	 * cascading FK from `product_barcodes`, so a blanket delete blows away
136	 * every learned barcode → product mapping the guard has built up.
137	 * Instead we upsert each remote row, then delete the ones the remote
138	 * no longer knows about.
139	 *
140	 * Per-row try/catch: one bad payload row used to abort the whole loop,
141	 * which left the local cache silently missing items further down the
142	 * list. Now we count failures and surface them so they can be diagnosed.
143	 */
144	export async function replaceCanonicalProducts(
145	  products: CanonicalProduct[],
146	): Promise<{ ok: number; failed: number; firstError?: string }> {
147	  const db = await getDb();
148	  let ok = 0;
149	  let failed = 0;
150	  let firstError: string | undefined;
151	  for (const p of products) {
152	    try {
153	      // Coerce fields the local schema marks NOT NULL — the server should
154	      // already enforce these, but a single bad row (e.g. unit migrated
155	      // from null) shouldn't drop the rest of the catalog.
156	      await upsertCanonicalProduct({
157	        product_id: String(p.product_id),
158	        canonical_name: String(p.canonical_name ?? ''),
159	        category: p.category ?? null,
160	        hsn_code: p.hsn_code ?? null,
161	        unit: p.unit ?? 'pcs',
162	        pack_size:
163	          typeof p.pack_size === 'number' && Number.isFinite(p.pack_size)
164	            ? p.pack_size
165	            : 1,
166	        has_barcode: p.has_barcode ? 1 : 0,
167	        primary_barcode: p.primary_barcode ?? null,
168	        updated_at: p.updated_at ?? Date.now(),
169	      });
170	      ok++;
171	    } catch (err) {
172	      failed++;
173	      if (!firstError) firstError = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
174	      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
```

1	import { Calendar, Check, X } from 'lucide-react-native';
2	import React, { useState } from 'react';
3	import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
4	import { CompactStepper, IconButton, radius, spacing, Text } from '../../../design';
5	import { useT } from '../../../i18n';
6	import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
7	import { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';
8	import { DatePickerModal } from './DatePickerModal';
9	
10	export type Batch = {
11	  /** ISO YYYY-MM-DD; null = "no expiry". */
12	  expiry: string | null;
13	  qty: number;
14	};
15	
16	function formatForDisplay(iso: string): string {
17	  const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');
18	  return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
19	}
20	
21	function toISO(d: Date): string {
22	  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
23	}
24	
25	/**
26	 * Edits a list of per-expiry batches for receiving / corrections. Each
27	 * row carries `{qty, expiry}`. Tapping the date opens the picker; the
28	 * checkbox toggles "no expiry" for that single row. Replaces the legacy
29	 * "different expiry per pack" toggle that exploded into N qty=1 rows
30	 * even when the guard had three groups, not ten packs.
31	 */
32	export function BatchEditor({
33	  batches,
34	  onChange,
35	  minDate,
36	  unit,
37	}: {
38	  batches: Batch[];
39	  onChange: (next: Batch[]) => void;
40	  minDate?: Date;
41	  /** Optional unit string, e.g. "g" — appears next to total. */
42	  unit?: string | null;
43	}) {
44	  const t = useT();
45	  const { palette } = useTheme();
46	  const [editingIdx, setEditingIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);
47	
48	  const total = batches.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.qty || 0), 0);
49	
50	  const update = (i: number, next: Partial<Batch>) => {
51	    onChange(batches.map((b, idx) => (idx === i ? { ...b, ...next } : b)));
52	  };
53	  const remove = (i: number) => {
54	    haptic.tap();
55	    onChange(batches.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i));
56	  };
57	  const add = () => {
58	    haptic.tap();
59	    // Seed new batch with qty=1 and copy last expiry as a hint —
60	    // common case is "another batch on the same day".
61	    const last = batches[batches.length - 1];
62	    onChange([...batches, { qty: 1, expiry: last?.expiry ?? null }]);
63	  };
64	
65	  return (
66	    <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
67	      <View
68	        style={{
69	          flexDirection: 'row',
70	          justifyContent: 'space-between',
71	          alignItems: 'baseline',
72	        }}
73	      >
74	        <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
75	          {t('batches').toUpperCase()}
76	        </Text>
77	        <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
78	          {t('totalLabel')}: {total}
79	          {unit ? ` ${unit}` : ''}
80	        </Text>
81	      </View>
82	
83	      {batches.map((b, i) => (
84	        <View
85	          key={i}
86	          style={[
87	            styles.row,
88	            {
89	              backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
90	              borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
91	            },
92	          ]}
93	        >
94	          <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: spacing.sm }}>
95	            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.sm }}>
96	              <Text variant="labelMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ minWidth: 32 }}>
97	                #{i + 1}
98	              </Text>
99	              <CompactStepper
100	                value={b.qty}
101	                onChange={(v) => update(i, { qty: v })}
102	                min={1}
103	              />
104	            </View>
105	
106	            {b.expiry === null ? (
107	              <Pressable
108	                onPress={() => {
109	                  haptic.tap();
110	                  // Tapping "no expiry" row re-opens the picker so it's easy
111	                  // to switch back. Seed with today.
112	                  update(i, { expiry: toISO(new Date()) });
113	                  setEditingIdx(i);
114	                }}
115	                style={[styles.dateRow, { borderColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}
116	              >
117	                <Calendar size={18} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2} />
118	                <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm }}>
119	                  {t('noExpiry')}
120	                </Text>
121	              </Pressable>
122	            ) : (
123	              <Pressable
124	                onPress={() => setEditingIdx(i)}
125	                style={[styles.dateRow, { borderColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}
126	              >
127	                <Calendar size={18} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2} />
128	                <Text
129	                  variant="bodyMedium"
130	                  color={palette.onSurface}
131	                  style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm, letterSpacing: 1 }}
132	                >
133	                  {formatForDisplay(b.expiry)}
134	                </Text>
135	              </Pressable>
136	            )}
137	
138	            <Pressable
139	              onPress={() => {
140	                haptic.tap();
141	                update(i, { expiry: b.expiry === null ? toISO(new Date()) : null });
142	              }}
143	              style={styles.noExpRow}
144	            >
145	              <View
146	                style={[
147	                  styles.checkbox,
148	                  {
149	                    backgroundColor: b.expiry === null ? palette.primary : 'transparent',
150	                    borderColor: b.expiry === null ? palette.primary : palette.outline,
151	                  },
152	                ]}
153	              >
154	                {b.expiry === null && <Check size={12} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={3} />}
155	              </View>
156	              <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
157	                {t('noExpiry')}
158	              </Text>
159	            </Pressable>
160	          </View>
161	
162	          {batches.length > 1 && (
163	            <IconButton Icon={X} onPress={() => remove(i)} />
164	          )}
165	        </View>
166	      ))}
167	
168	      <Pressable
169	        onPress={add}
170	        style={[
171	          styles.addBtn,
172	          { borderColor: palette.outline, backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLow },
173	        ]}
174	      >
175	        <Text variant="titleMedium" color={palette.primary}>
176	          {t('addBatch')}
177	        </Text>
178	      </Pressable>
179	
180	      {editingIdx != null && (
181	        <DatePickerModal
182	          visible={editingIdx != null}
183	          value={batches[editingIdx]?.expiry ? new Date(batches[editingIdx].expiry as string) : null}
184	          minimumDate={minDate}
185	          onSelect={(d) => {
186	            update(editingIdx, { expiry: toISO(d) });
187	          }}
188	          onDismiss={() => setEditingIdx(null)}
189	        />
190	      )}
191	    </View>
192	  );
193	}
194	
195	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
196	  row: {
197	    flexDirection: 'row',
198	    alignItems: 'flex-start',
199	    padding: spacing.md,
200	    borderRadius: radius.md,
201	    borderWidth: 1,
202	    gap: spacing.sm,
203	  },
204	  dateRow: {
205	    flexDirection: 'row',
206	    alignItems: 'center',
207	    paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,
208	    paddingVertical: spacing.sm,
209	    borderRadius: radius.md,
210	    borderWidth: 1,
211	    minHeight: 44,
212	  },
213	  noExpRow: {
214	    flexDirection: 'row',
215	    alignItems: 'center',
216	    gap: spacing.sm,
217	  },
218	  checkbox: {
219	    width: 18,
220	    height: 18,
221	    borderRadius: 4,
222	    borderWidth: 2,
223	    alignItems: 'center',
224	    justifyContent: 'center',
225	  },
226	  addBtn: {
227	    paddingVertical: spacing.md,
228	    borderRadius: radius.md,
229	    borderWidth: 1.5,
230	    borderStyle: 'dashed',
231	    alignItems: 'center',
232	  },
233	});

  "new_string": "export function BatchEditor({\n  batches,\n  onChange,\n  minDate,\n  unit,\n  dispenseMode = 'pack',\n}: {\n  batches: Batch[];\n  onChange: (next: Batch[]) => void;\n  minDate?: Date;\n  /** Optional unit string, e.g. \"g\" — appears next to total. */\n  unit?: string | null;\n  /** 'pack' → integer CompactStepper. 'divisible' → decimal TextInput\n   *  in base units (e.g. kg, ml). */\n  dispenseMode?: 'pack' | 'divisible';\n}) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const [editingIdx, setEditingIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const divisible = dispenseMode === 'divisible';\n\n  const total = batches.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.qty || 0), 0);\n  const totalDisplay = divisible\n    ? (Math.round(total * 1000) / 1000).toString()\n    : String(total);\n\n  const update = (i: number, next: Partial<Batch>) => {\n    onChange(batches.map((b, idx) => (idx === i ? { ...b, ...next } : b)));\n  };\n  const remove = (i: number) => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    onChange(batches.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i));\n  };\n  const add = () => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    const last = batches[batches.length - 1];\n    onChange([...batches, { qty: divisible ? 0 : 1, expiry: last?.expiry ?? null }]);\n  };"
}

12	export async function findProduct(barcode: string): Promise<Product | null> {
13	  const db = await getDb();
14	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<Product>(
15	    'SELECT * FROM products WHERE barcode = ?',
16	    [barcode]
17	  );

21	export async function upsertProduct(p: Omit<Product, 'updated_at'>): Promise<Product> {
22	  const db = await getDb();
23	  const ts = now();
24	  await db.runAsync(
25	    `INSERT INTO products (barcode, name, category, unit, pack_size, updated_at)
26	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
27	     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET
28	       name=excluded.name,
29	       category=excluded.category,
30	       unit=excluded.unit,
31	       pack_size=excluded.pack_size,
32	       updated_at=excluded.updated_at`,
33	    [p.barcode, p.name, p.category, p.unit, p.pack_size, ts]
34	  );
35	  return { ...p, updated_at: ts };
36	}
37	
38	export async function listProducts(limit = 200): Promise<Product[]> {
39	  const db = await getDb();
40	  return db.getAllAsync<Product>(

The translation to\n  // base units (e.g. 3 packs × 5 kg → 15 kg lot qty) happens at submit.\n  const [batches, setBatches] = useState<Batch[]>([{ qty: 1, expiry: null }]);\n  const qty = useMemo(() => batches.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.qty || 0), 0), [batches]);"

  "new_string": "  const [dispenseMode, setDispenseMode] = useState<'pack' | 'divisible'>('pack');\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    (async () => {\n      setProduct(await findProduct(barcode));\n      const stockRow = await findStock(barcode);\n      if (stockRow?.dispense_mode === 'divisible') setDispenseMode('divisible');\n      // If we have a productId from catalog, look up order item by product_id\n      if (productId) {\n        const byProduct = await findOpenItemByProductId(productId);\n        setItem(byProduct as OrderItem | null);\n      } else {\n        setItem(await findOpenItemByBarcode(barcode));\n      }\n    })();\n  }, [barcode, productId]);\n\n  /** Convert a \"packs received\" integer into the units stored in lots:\n   *  packs for pack-mode; packs × pack_size (base units) for divisible. */\n  const toLotQty = (packsQty: number): number => {\n    if (dispenseMode !== 'divisible') return packsQty;\n    const ps = resolvedPackSize && resolvedPackSize > 0 ? resolvedPackSize : 1;\n    return packsQty * ps;\n  };"
}
