104	  const submitAll = useCallback(async () => {
105	    const session = getSession();
106	    const performedBy = session?.guardId ?? null;
107	    const performedByName = session?.guardName ?? null;
108	
109	    // Make sure our local mirror of open orders is current before we try
110	    // to resolve productId → order_item_id. Without this, the very first
111	    // scan of the day misses the order link and the Received counter
112	    // stays at 0 until the next periodic sync.
113	    await syncOrders().catch(() => {});
114	
115	    for (const item of items) {
116	      // Prefer the canonical-product link (set when guard picked from the
117	      // catalog); fall back to barcode lookup for legacy barcoded items.
118	      const orderItem = item.productId
119	        ? await findOpenItemByProductId(item.productId).catch(() => null)
120	        : await findOpenItemByBarcode(item.barcode).catch(() => null);
121	
122	      // One receipt per batch so each distinct expiry survives end-to-end
123	      // (outbox → backend → dashboard → Excel).
124	      const batches = item.batches.length > 0 ? item.batches : [{ qty: item.qty, expiry: null }];
125	      for (const batch of batches) {
126	        const receiptId = `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`;
127	        await enqueue('receipt', {
128	          id: receiptId,
129	          order_id: orderItem?.order_id ?? null,
130	          order_item_id: orderItem?.id ?? null,
131	          product_id: item.productId ?? null,
132	          barcode: item.barcode,
133	          product_name: item.name,
134	          qty: batch.qty,
135	          expiry_date: batch.expiry,
136	          flagged: false,
137	          scanned_at: now(),
138	          performed_by: performedBy,
139	          performed_by_name: performedByName,
140	        });
141	
142	        // Mirror the lot locally so EditStock / Dispense see the new
143	        // batch immediately, without waiting for the next sync round-trip.
144	        await addLotLocal(item.barcode, batch.expiry, batch.qty);
145	
146	        if (batch.expiry) {
147	          await trackExpiry({
148	            barcode: item.barcode,
149	            productName: item.name,
150	            expiryDate: batch.expiry,
151	            qty: batch.qty,
152	            receiptId,
153	            performedBy,
154	            performedByName,
155	          });
156	        }
157	      }
158	
159	      // Update local order tracking if applicable (once per item — uses summed qty)
160	      if (orderItem) {
161	        await addReceivedQty(orderItem.id, item.qty);
162	      }
163	
164	      // Barcode learning — remember this real barcode → canonical product
165	      // so the next scan auto-resolves to the right item without the
166	      // CatalogPicker. Skip synthetic "catalog_*" barcodes from picks
167	      // with no physical barcode.
168	      if (item.productId && !item.barcode.startsWith('catalog_')) {
169	        await learnBarcode(item.barcode, item.productId, 'scan');
170	        await enqueue('learn_barcode', {
171	          barcode: item.barcode,
172	          product_id: item.productId,
173	        }).catch(() => {});
174	      }
175	
176	      // Roll qty into on-hand stock (once per item — total qty across batches)
177	      const existing = await findStock(item.barcode);
178	      if (existing) {
179	        await adjustOnHand(item.barcode, item.qty);
180	      } else {
181	        await upsertStock({
182	          barcode: item.barcode,
183	          name: item.name,
184	          category: item.category,
185	          unit: item.unit,
186	          pack_size: item.packSize ?? null,
187	          on_hand: item.qty,
188	          threshold: 0,
189	        });
190	      }
191	    }
192	
193	    // Try to sync immediately, don't block if offline
194	    flushOnce().catch(() => {});
195	
196	    // Clear session after successful submission
197	    setItems([]);
198	    setLastExpiry(null);
199	  }, [items]);

1	import { Minus, Plus, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react-native';
2	import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
3	import { Pressable, StyleSheet, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';
4	import { useTheme } from '../theme';
5	import { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';
6	import { Text } from './Text';
7	import { spacing, type as typo } from './tokens';
8	
9	type Props = {
10	  value: number;
11	  onChange: (n: number) => void;
12	  min?: number;
13	  max?: number;
14	  /** Allow fractional values (e.g. 0.5 kg). +/- still nudge by 1. */
15	  decimal?: boolean;
16	};
17	
18	/**
19	 * Big stepper for low-literacy users. Plus/minus circles flank a tappable
20	 * display: tapping the number swaps it for an inline TextInput so power
21	 * users can type the qty directly. Commits on blur or submit.
22	 */
23	export function QtyStepper({ value, onChange, min = 1, max, decimal = true }: Props) {
24	  const { palette } = useTheme();
25	  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
26	  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(String(value));
27	  const inputRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
28	
29	  useEffect(() => {
30	    if (!editing) setDraft(String(value));
31	  }, [value, editing]);
32	
33	  const clamp = (n: number) => {
34	    let out = n;
35	    if (Number.isNaN(out)) out = min;
36	    if (out < min) out = min;
37	    if (max !== undefined && out > max) out = max;
38	    return out;
39	  };
40	
41	  const bump = (delta: number) => {
42	    haptic.tap();
43	    onChange(clamp(value + delta));
44	  };
45	
46	  const startEdit = () => {
47	    haptic.tap();
48	    setDraft(String(value));
49	    setEditing(true);
50	    requestAnimationFrame(() => inputRef.current?.focus());
51	  };
52	
53	  /**
54	   * Propagate every keystroke to the parent so a submit handler that
55	   * fires before our `onBlur` (e.g. tapping a Confirm button) still sees
56	   * the latest qty. The local `draft` keeps the literal text so partial
57	   * states like "" don't snap the display while typing.
58	   */
59	  const parseValue = (text: string) => decimal ? parseFloat(text) : parseInt(text, 10);
60	  const validText = (text: string) =>
61	    decimal ? /^-?\d*\.?\d*$/.test(text) : /^-?\d*$/.test(text);
62	
63	  const onTextChange = (text: string) => {
64	    if (!validText(text)) return; // reject stray characters
65	    setDraft(text);
66	    if (text.trim() === '' || text === '.' || text === '-') {
67	      // Don't push min on every backspace; wait for commit.
68	      return;
69	    }
70	    const parsed = parseValue(text);
71	    if (!Number.isNaN(parsed)) onChange(clamp(parsed));
72	  };
73	
74	  const commit = () => {
75	    const next = clamp(parseValue(draft));
76	    onChange(next);
77	    setDraft(String(next));
78	    setEditing(false);
79	  };
80	
81	  return (
82	    <View style={styles.row}>
83	      <StepperButton Icon={Minus} onPress={() => bump(-1)} bg={palette.primary} fg={palette.onPrimary} ripple={palette.outlineVariant} />
84	      {editing ? (
85	        <TextInput
86	          ref={inputRef}
87	          value={draft}
88	          onChangeText={onTextChange}
89	          onBlur={commit}
90	          onSubmitEditing={commit}
91	          keyboardType={decimal ? "decimal-pad" : "number-pad"}
92	          selectTextOnFocus
93	          returnKeyType="done"
94	          style={[
95	            typo.displayLarge,
96	            styles.qtyText,
97	            { color: palette.onSurface, borderBottomColor: palette.primary },

112	    </View>
113	  );
114	}

1	import React, { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
2	import { StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
3	import { CompactStepper, radius, spacing, Text } from '../../../design';
4	import { useT } from '../../../i18n';
5	import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
6	
7	export type LotAvailability = {
8	  /** ISO YYYY-MM-DD; null = no expiry. */
9	  expiry: string | null;
10	  /** Total currently in stock for this lot. */
11	  available: number;
12	};
13	
14	export type LotAllocation = LotAvailability & {
15	  /** How much the guard is taking from this lot. 0 = none. */
16	  take: number;
17	};
18	
19	function formatForDisplay(iso: string | null): string {
20	  if (iso === null) return '—';
21	  const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');
22	  return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
23	}
24	
25	/**
26	 * Suggest a FEFO allocation: take from the earliest-expiry lot first.
27	 * Returns one entry per available lot (zero `take` if not used). Lots
28	 * are expected in FEFO order from the caller.
29	 */
30	export function suggestFEFO(
31	  lots: LotAvailability[],
32	  totalQty: number,
33	): LotAllocation[] {
34	  let remaining = totalQty;
35	  return lots.map((l) => {
36	    const take = Math.max(0, Math.min(l.available, remaining));
37	    remaining -= take;
38	    return { ...l, take };
39	  });
40	}
41	
42	/**
43	 * Per-lot qty picker for dispense. Lots are listed in FEFO order with
44	 * the earliest highlighted as "suggested" — this is the **soft nudge**:
45	 * we record what the guard actually picked rather than enforcing FEFO,
46	 * so accounting matches physical reality. Guards can adjust each row;
47	 * the editor enforces the per-lot ceiling so they can't take more than
48	 * exists in any batch.
49	 *
50	 * The component is fully controlled — caller owns `lots` and the
51	 * resulting allocation via `onChange`. Total taken is derived from
52	 * `lots.reduce((s, l) => s + l.take, 0)` and displayed at the bottom.
53	 */
54	export function LotPicker({
55	  lots,
56	  onChange,
57	  desiredQty,
58	  unit,
59	}: {
60	  /** Allocation rows. Order = FEFO from caller. */
61	  lots: LotAllocation[];
62	  onChange: (next: LotAllocation[]) => void;
63	  /** What the guard initially asked for. Used for the "Taken N of M" badge. */
64	  desiredQty: number;
65	  unit?: string | null;
66	}) {
67	  const t = useT();
68	  const { palette } = useTheme();
69	
70	  const total = useMemo(() => lots.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.take || 0), 0), [lots]);
71	
72	  // Auto-seed allocation with FEFO when the parent passes the freshly-
73	  // loaded availability without any takes set. Idempotent — caller
74	  // controls reseeding by changing the `lots` reference.
75	  useEffect(() => {
76	    const noneAllocated = lots.every((l) => l.take === 0);
77	    if (noneAllocated && desiredQty > 0) {
78	      onChange(suggestFEFO(lots, desiredQty));
79	    }
80	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
81	  }, [lots.length]);
82	
83	  if (lots.length === 0) {
84	    return (
85	      <View>
86	        <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
87	          —
88	        </Text>
89	      </View>
90	    );
91	  }
92	
93	  return (
94	    <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
95	      <View
96	        style={{
97	          flexDirection: 'row',
98	          justifyContent: 'space-between',
99	          alignItems: 'baseline',
100	        }}
101	      >
102	        <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
103	          {t('pickFromBatch').toUpperCase()}
104	        </Text>
105	        <Text
106	          variant="bodyMedium"
107	          color={total === desiredQty ? palette.primary : palette.onSurfaceVariant}
108	        >
109	          {t('takenTotal')}: {total} {t('ofTotal')} {desiredQty}
110	          {unit ? ` ${unit}` : ''}
111	        </Text>
112	      </View>
113	
114	      {lots.map((lot, i) => {
115	        const isFefoSuggested = i === 0;
116	        return (
117	          <View
118	            key={`${lot.expiry ?? '__none__'}-${i}`}
119	            style={[
120	              styles.row,
121	              {
122	                backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
123	                borderColor:
124	                  isFefoSuggested && lot.take > 0
125	                    ? palette.primary
126	                    : palette.outlineVariant,
127	                borderWidth: isFefoSuggested && lot.take > 0 ? 2 : 1,
128	              },
129	            ]}
130	          >
131	            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
132	              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.sm }}>
133	                <Text variant="titleMedium" color={palette.onSurface} style={{ letterSpacing: 1 }}>
134	                  {formatForDisplay(lot.expiry)}
135	                </Text>
136	                {isFefoSuggested && (
137	                  <Text variant="labelMedium" color={palette.primary}>
138	                    ← {t('suggested')}
139	                  </Text>
140	                )}
141	              </View>
142	              <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: 2 }}>
143	                {lot.available} {t('inStock')}
144	              </Text>
145	            </View>
146	            <CompactStepper
147	              value={lot.take}
148	              onChange={(v) => {
149	                const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(lot.available, v));
150	                onChange(lots.map((l, idx) => (idx === i ? { ...l, take: clamped } : l)));
151	              }}
152	              min={0}
153	              max={lot.available}
154	            />
155	          </View>
156	        );
157	      })}
158	    </View>
159	  );
160	}
161	
162	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
163	  row: {
164	    flexDirection: 'row',
165	    alignItems: 'center',
166	    padding: spacing.md,
167	    borderRadius: radius.md,
168	    gap: spacing.md,
169	  },
170	});
171

147	        if (cancelled) return;
148	        const remoteQty = Number(remote.on_hand);
149	        setVerifiedOnHand(remoteQty);
150	        setVerifyStatus('ok');
151	        // Clamp qty to the verified ceiling. If the user had a stale
152	        // higher qty in mind (e.g. local cache said 10, server says 7),
153	        // drop to the new max so they can't accidentally submit over.
154	        const clampedQty = qty > remoteQty ? Math.max(1, remoteQty) : qty;
155	        if (clampedQty !== qty) setQty(clampedQty);
156	
157	        // Build the lot picker state. Prefer the remote lots (verified
158	        // truth); fall back to the local lots cache when the server
159	        // returned no breakdown. Final fallback for legacy rows that
160	        // never received a lot write (existed before the lots
161	        // migration): synthesise a single lot from on_hand +
162	        // nearest_expiry so dispense still works on existing inventory.
163	        const remoteLots = remote.lots ?? [];

173	            qty: remoteQty,

182	      } catch (err) {
183	        if (cancelled) return;
184	        // 404: the server has no record of this barcode (might be a
185	        // device-only item never received server-side). Treat as
186	        // "absent" — block dispense; you can't dispense what the
187	        // server doesn't know exists.
188	        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
189	          setVerifyStatus('absent');
190	          return;
191	        }
192	        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) return;
193	        setVerifyStatus(isOfflineError(err) ? 'offline' : 'error');
194	      } finally {
195	        clearTimeout(t);
196	      }
197	    })();
198	    return () => { cancelled = true; clearTimeout(t); controller.abort(); };
199	    // intentionally NOT in deps: `qty` would re-run the JIT on every
200	    // stepper tap; only refire when the selected barcode changes.
201	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
202	  }, [selected?.barcode]);
203	
204	  const results = useMemo(() => {
205	    if (selected) return [];
206	    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
207	    return all
208	      .filter((r) => {
209	        if (category !== 'All' && r.category !== category) return false;
210	        if (statusFilter !== 'All') {
211	          const s = statusFor(r);
212	          if (statusFilter === 'In Stock' && s !== 'ok') return false;
213	          if (statusFilter === 'Low' && s !== 'low') return false;
214	          if (statusFilter === 'Out' && s !== 'out') return false;
215	        }
216	        if (!q) return true;
217	        return r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.barcode.includes(q) || (r.category ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
218	      })
219	      .slice(0, 30);
```

  "new_string": "/** Format the pack-size hint that sits under the on-hand count.\n *  pack_size null/1 → just the unit (\"g\"); otherwise \"× 100 g\".\n *  Divisible products store on_hand in the base unit, so the hint is\n *  just the unit (the count already reads \"47.5 kg\"). */\nfunction packSizeLabel(row: StockRow): string {\n  const unit = row.unit ?? '';\n  if (row.dispense_mode === 'divisible') return unit;\n  if (row.pack_size == null || row.pack_size === 1) return unit;\n  // Trim trailing zero on whole numbers (100 not 100.0) but keep \"1.5\".\n  const ps = Number.isInteger(row.pack_size) ? String(row.pack_size) : String(row.pack_size);\n  return unit ? `× ${ps} ${unit}` : `× ${ps}`;\n}"
}

2071	function CatalogProductRow({ product: p }: { product: CanonicalProduct }) {
2072	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
2073	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
2074	  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
2075	
2076	  // Edit-mode draft. Re-seeded from `p` whenever editing starts so that
2077	  // a cancel-then-reopen reads the latest server state, not whatever the
2078	  // admin previously typed.
2079	  const [draft, setDraft] = useState({
2080	    canonical_name: p.canonical_name,
2081	    category: p.category ?? "",
2082	    hsn_code: p.hsn_code ?? "",
2083	    unit: p.unit,
2084	    pack_size: p.pack_size,
2085	  });
2086	
2087	  function startEdit() {
2088	    setDraft({
2089	      canonical_name: p.canonical_name,
2090	      category: p.category ?? "",
2091	      hsn_code: p.hsn_code ?? "",
2092	      unit: p.unit,
2093	      pack_size: p.pack_size,
2094	    });
2095	    setEditing(true);
2096	    // Open the expansion alongside so admins manage barcodes + vendor
2097	    // aliases in the same flow as the inline-row edit. Saves the "edit
2098	    // → save → click chevron → edit barcode → click chevron" dance.
2099	    setOpen(true);
2100	  }
2101	
2102	  // Cancel = leave edit mode AND collapse the expansion that startEdit
2103	  // forced open. If the admin had the expansion open before they
2104	  // clicked Edit, that state is lost on cancel — acceptable because
2105	  // there's no clean way to distinguish "open because Edit forced it"
2106	  // from "open because admin clicked the chevron".
2107	  function cancelEdit() {
2108	    setEditing(false);
2109	    setOpen(false);
2110	  }
2111

> AGENT

All eight tasks done. Summary of what shipped:

**Engine (`office-ops-engine/`)**
- `postgres-schema.ts` — added `dispense_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pack'` to `inventory_products` and `canonical_products` (idempotent).
- `db.ts` — types extended; `STOCK_SELECT` returns `COALESCE(cp.dispense_mode, ip.dispense_mode, 'pack')` so every stock read carries the mode. New `canonicalProductHasStock()` guard helper.
- `routes.ts` — `/products` and `/canonical-products` POST accept `dispense_mode`; PATCH `/canonical-products/:id` accepts it with a 409 if stock/lots exist. Corrections clamp no longer rounds (preserves decimal qty).

**Field-app**
- Schema v9 migration adds `dispense_mode` to `products`, `canonical_products`, `stock_levels` (all default `'pack'`).
- `api.ts` / `syncService.ts` / `stock.ts` / `catalog.ts` mirror `dispense_mode` end-to-end.
- `BatchEditor` swaps `CompactStepper` → decimal `TextInput` when `dispenseMode === 'divisible'`; total displays with fractional units.
- `LotPicker` gains a `decimal` prop that does the same per-lot.
- `DispenseScreen` flips `QtyStepper` to `decimal` mode and adjusts the on-hand display for divisible products.
- `ReceivingScreen` and `OrderSessionContext.submitAll` keep the receiving UI integer-packs but multiply by `pack_size` to write base-unit qty to lots/stock when divisible.
- `StockScreen` shows just the unit (no "× pack_size") for divisible.

**Dashboard (`ops-dashboard/`)**
- `CanonicalProduct` type + create/patch payloads include `dispense_mode`.
- Catalog tab: create form has a "Divisible" checkbox; row expansion shows current mode + lets admin flip in edit mode; row table shows a small `Divisible` badge next to pack_size.

Existing pack-mode SKUs (all 53 in the catalog) are unaffected — default `'pack'` matches today's behaviour; UI changes only render for SKUs an admin explicitly flips.

Not committed (per repo rule).

> DEVELOPER

issues, neither in the item creation page, stocks page, nor the dispense page in the field app I am able to put decimal values (this is with already mapped items in the catalog), also the info shown is wrong, in a lot of item the unit is gram, the unit value if 500 gram, if I add 2 of that product it shows, 2g, instead it should either show me 2 x 500 g, or 1000 g (500x2) . check everywhere and fix it, and tell me where and how the pack or unit flow will work?

> AGENT

You've actually flagged two distinct issues stacked together. Let me separate them, fix the display bug across the field-app, and then explain the flow so you can decide if the design needs to change.

## The two issues

**A. Display bug (real bug, existing behavior).** Items with `unit=g, pack_size=500` are pack-mode. `on_hand=2` *means* "2 packs of 500g". Every screen should render it as `2 × 500 g` or `1000 g` — never "2 g". If you see "2 g", a screen is concatenating `qty + unit` without consulting `pack_size`. That's a bug regardless of divisible mode.

**B. Decimals aren't available on existing items (by design, but you're asking why).** Per the design we agreed on: each SKU's `dispense_mode` is set once (in the dashboard, on the catalog product), and existing items default to `'pack'`. So your current 53 catalog items will all reject decimals until an admin flips them. **You currently have no way to do that from the field-app** — registration doesn't expose the toggle.

Let me first audit and fix issue A, then fix issue B properly.

The bug is in `BatchEditor`'s total line: for `pack_size=500, unit=g, qty=2 packs`, it renders "Total: 2 g". Same applies if anything else passes raw `unit` without thinking about packs. Let me fix `BatchEditor` to take `packSize` and render correctly:

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "export function BatchEditor({\n  batches,\n  onChange,\n  minDate,\n  unit,\n  dispenseMode = 'pack',\n}: {\n  batches: Batch[];\n  onChange: (next: Batch[]) => void;\n  minDate?: Date;\n  /** Optional unit string, e.g. \"g\" — appears next to total. */\n  unit?: string | null;\n  /** 'pack' → integer CompactStepper. 'divisible' → decimal TextInput\n   *  in base units (e.g. kg, ml). */\n  dispenseMode?: 'pack' | 'divisible';\n}) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const [editingIdx, setEditingIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const divisible = dispenseMode === 'divisible';\n\n  const total = batches.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.qty || 0), 0);\n  const totalDisplay = divisible\n    ? (Math.round(total * 1000) / 1000).toString()\n    : String(total);",
  "new_string": "export function BatchEditor({\n  batches,\n  onChange,\n  minDate,\n  unit,\n  packSize,\n  dispenseMode = 'pack',\n}: {\n  batches: Batch[];\n  onChange: (next: Batch[]) => void;\n  minDate?: Date;\n  /** Base unit string, e.g. \"g\". */\n  unit?: string | null;\n  /** Pack size — needed to render \"2 × 500 g\" (pack mode) vs raw unit\n   *  (divisible). When omitted, falls back to bare qty + unit. */\n  packSize?: number | null;\n  /** 'pack' → integer CompactStepper. 'divisible' → decimal TextInput\n   *  in base units (e.g. kg, ml). */\n  dispenseMode?: 'pack' | 'divisible';\n}) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const [editingIdx, setEditingIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const divisible = dispenseMode === 'divisible';\n\n  const total = batches.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.qty || 0), 0);\n  // Pack mode + meaningful pack_size: \"2 × 500 g\" (avoids the \"2 g\" bug\n  // when qty is really pack count and unit is the base unit).\n  // Divisible mode: qty IS the base-unit total → \"2.5 kg\".\n  // Otherwise: \"2 g\" / \"2\" depending on what we know.\n  const ps = packSize ?? null;\n  const usePackForm = !divisible && ps != null && ps !== 1;\n  const totalDisplay = divisible\n    ? (Math.round(total * 1000) / 1000).toString()\n    : String(total);\n  const totalSuffix = usePackForm\n    ? ` × ${ps}${unit ? ` ${unit}` : ''}`\n    : unit ? ` ${unit}` : '';"
}

> AGENT

Clean. Now let me clearly explain what changed, and how the pack/divisible flow works end-to-end.

## What was fixed

1. **BatchEditor total bug (the "2 g" issue)** — `BatchEditor.tsx` now takes `packSize` and renders the total as `2 × 500 g` (pack mode) instead of `2 g`. Both ReceivingScreen and EditStockScreen now pass `packSize` in.
2. **Field-app registration didn't expose dispense_mode** — `RegisterProductScreen.tsx` now shows a "TRACKING MODE" chip pair (`Whole packs` / `Divisible`) whenever you're registering a brand-new product with `pack_size > 1`. The chosen mode flows through the outbox into the server. (It's hidden for catalog picks and for `pack_size=1` SKUs — those have no meaningful "subdivide" option.)

## How the pack / unit / divisible flow works

There are **two orthogonal facts** per SKU and they control two different things:

| Field | What it is | Set by | Affects |
|---|---|---|---|
| `unit` | The base measurement word: `g`, `kg`, `ml`, `pcs`, `pack` | Admin at catalog creation; guard at field registration | Display only — appears next to qty |
| `pack_size` | How many base units are in one physical pack | Same | Display formula + qty conversion when divisible |
| `dispense_mode` | `pack` (integer pack count) or `divisible` (decimal base units) | **Admin in dashboard** for existing catalog items; **guard in field-app registration** for brand-new SKUs | Whether decimals are allowed; how qty is interpreted |

### Pack mode (the default; all 53 existing SKUs)

- `qty=2, pack_size=500, unit=g` ⇒ "2 packs, each contains 500 g". Display: **`2 × 500 g`** or "2 packs (1000 g total)".
- Receive in packs (integer), dispense in packs (integer).
- `on_hand=2` means 2 packs, not 2 grams.

### Divisible mode (opt-in per SKU)

- `qty=2.5, unit=kg` ⇒ "2.5 kg on hand". `pack_size` is metadata for receiving only.
- Receive: still ask "how many packs received?" (integer), then the app multiplies by `pack_size` and stores **2 × 5 kg = 10 kg** in the lot.
- Dispense: accepts decimal in base units ("dispense 1.5 kg").
- `on_hand` is in base units (kg/ml/g) — so for 47.5 kg of flour the display is just `47.5 kg`, no `× pack_size`.

### Who can flip the mode for an existing item

This is the part you bumped into:

- **Brand-new SKU registered from the field-app** → chip is right there on the registration screen. The user picks at the moment of creation.
- **Catalog SKU created from the dashboard** → admin checks "Divisible" in the dashboard's Catalog tab (in the new product form, or in the per-row edit panel I just added).
- **Existing SKU with stock** → can't flip in place. The server returns `409` if stock or any open lot exists. Reason: pack-mode and divisible interpret `qty` differently, so flipping with stock alive would silently corrupt every number. To flip: zero out stock first (dispense or correct to 0), then change the mode, then re-receive.

### What to do for your current items

For the 4 SKUs that should be divisible (cornflakes, turmeric, salt, toor dal — all `pack_size=1, unit=kg`): they already store qty in `kg` because `pack_size=1`, so flipping them to `divisible` is *cosmetically safe* — the existing `on_hand` values already mean "kg". But the server still blocks the flip while stock > 0 as a precaution. Two clean ways to do it:

1. **Right now, before there's much stock**: zero them out and flip via the dashboard. One-time pain.
2. **Loosen the rule for `pack_size=1` SKUs**: I can change the server guard so it only blocks flips when `pack_size != 1` (because for `pack_size=1` the unit interpretation is the same in both modes). Want me to do that?

Type-check clean across all three subrepos. Not committed.

  "new_string": "  /** Re-suggest FEFO whenever the user bumps the big \"How many?\" stepper.\n   *  This intentionally overwrites manual per-lot edits — the per-lot\n   *  picker is for fine-tuning AFTER the total is set.\n   *\n   *  When the user instead drives the per-lot picker, we sync `qty` to\n   *  the new sum (see syncQtyFromAllocation). That sync would otherwise\n   *  trigger this effect and overwrite their lot edit; the ref below\n   *  swallows exactly one re-suggest pass to break the loop. */\n  const skipNextFefoRecalc = useRef(false);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (availability.length === 0) return;\n    if (skipNextFefoRecalc.current) {\n      skipNextFefoRecalc.current = false;\n      return;\n    }\n    setAllocation(suggestFEFO(availability, qty));\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [qty]);\n\n  /** Per-lot edit handler — keeps the \"How many?\" stepper in lockstep\n   *  with the sum of per-lot takes. Without this, the two surfaces drift:\n   *  the upper stepper says \"1\" while the picker says \"Taken 4 of 1\". */\n  const onAllocationChange = useCallback(\n    (next: typeof allocation) => {\n      const sum = next.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.take || 0), 0);\n      if (sum !== qty) {\n        skipNextFefoRecalc.current = true;\n        setQty(sum);\n      }\n      setAllocation(next);\n    },\n    [qty, allocation],\n  );"
}

> AGENT

Clean. Here's the new behavior with your 4-pack × 500 g example:

| State | Upper stepper | LotPicker shows |
|---|---|---|
| Initial (qty=1) | **1** | "Taken 1 × 500 g / 4 × 500 g in stock" |
| Bump upper to 4 | **4** | "Taken 4 × 500 g / 4 × 500 g in stock" (FEFO across lots) |
| Manually +1 a lot stepper | **synced to new sum** | "Taken N × 500 g / 4 × 500 g in stock" |

Two fixes:
1. **State sync** — when you tap +/− on a lot row, the new sum writes back to `qty` via `onAllocationChange`, and a one-shot ref-flag prevents the upper change from re-running FEFO and overwriting your edit.
2. **Ceiling visibility** — the LotPicker header now says `Taken N / TOTAL_AVAILABLE in stock` instead of `Taken N of REQUESTED`. You always see how much really exists.

Type-check clean. Not committed.

> DEVELOPER

I think it is becoming congested, let just show the data differently? what do you suggest?

> AGENT

Agreed — three "× 500 g"s on one screen is too much. The unit info is already on the product card up top; we don't need to repeat it inside the picker.

A few options for the LotPicker — pick one:

**Option A: bare counts in the picker, units stay on the product card.**
```
Product card:    4 × 500 g on hand
HOW MANY?        [ − 2 + ]

PICK FROM BATCH                       2 / 4 packs
15 Aug 2026   ← suggested             1 of 1  [ − 1 + ]
10 Sep 2026                           1 of 2  [ − 1 + ]
05 Nov 2026                           0 of 1  [ − 0 + ]
```
Cleanest. Each lot row shows just "X of Y" packs. The header sums the same in plain numbers.

**Option B: keep units but pull them out of the running line.**
```
PICK FROM BATCH                       2 / 4
15 Aug 2026   ← suggested             1 / 1 pack  [−1+]
10 Sep 2026                           1 / 2 packs [−1+]
```
Still names the unit per row but drops the "× 500 g" repetition — one word, not three.

**Option C: progress bar instead of a number line.**
```
PICK FROM BATCH                       ▓▓░░  2/4
[date row · stepper]
```
Visual; less text to parse. Probably overkill.

My recommendation: **Option A**. It matches your low-literacy constraint — fewer symbols, identical math everywhere, units handled exactly once on the product card. The "× 500 g" stays where it matters (the hero card), and the picker becomes a pure counting game.

Want me to apply A?

> DEVELOPER

okay option A, but make the info more noticeable and good to look at.

1	import React, { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
2	import { StyleSheet, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';
3	import { CompactStepper, radius, spacing, Text } from '../../../design';
4	import { useT } from '../../../i18n';
5	import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
6	
7	export type LotAvailability = {
8	  /** ISO YYYY-MM-DD; null = no expiry. */
9	  expiry: string | null;
10	  /** Total currently in stock for this lot. */
11	  available: number;
12	};
13	
14	export type LotAllocation = LotAvailability & {
15	  /** How much the guard is taking from this lot. 0 = none. */
16	  take: number;
17	};
18	
19	function formatForDisplay(iso: string | null): string {
20	  if (iso === null) return '—';
21	  const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');
22	  return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
23	}
24	
25	/**
26	 * Suggest a FEFO allocation: take from the earliest-expiry lot first.
27	 * Returns one entry per available lot (zero `take` if not used). Lots
28	 * are expected in FEFO order from the caller.
29	 */
30	export function suggestFEFO(
31	  lots: LotAvailability[],
32	  totalQty: number,
33	): LotAllocation[] {
34	  let remaining = totalQty;
35	  return lots.map((l) => {
36	    const take = Math.max(0, Math.min(l.available, remaining));
37	    remaining -= take;
38	    return { ...l, take };
39	  });
40	}
41	
42	/**
43	 * Per-lot qty picker for dispense. Lots are listed in FEFO order with
44	 * the earliest highlighted as "suggested" — this is the **soft nudge**:
45	 * we record what the guard actually picked rather than enforcing FEFO,
46	 * so accounting matches physical reality. Guards can adjust each row;
47	 * the editor enforces the per-lot ceiling so they can't take more than
48	 * exists in any batch.
49	 *
50	 * The component is fully controlled — caller owns `lots` and the
51	 * resulting allocation via `onChange`. Total taken is derived from
52	 * `lots.reduce((s, l) => s + l.take, 0)` and displayed at the bottom.
53	 */
54	export function LotPicker({
55	  lots,
56	  onChange,
57	  desiredQty,
58	  unit,
59	  packSize,
60	  decimal = false,
61	}: {
62	  /** Allocation rows. Order = FEFO from caller. */
63	  lots: LotAllocation[];
64	  onChange: (next: LotAllocation[]) => void;
65	  /** What the guard initially asked for. Used for the "Taken N of M" badge. */
66	  desiredQty: number;
67	  unit?: string | null;
68	  /** Pack size — needed to render "2 × 500 g" (pack mode) vs raw unit
69	   *  (divisible). When omitted, falls back to bare qty + unit. */
70	  packSize?: number | null;
71	  /** When true, per-lot input accepts decimals (divisible products). */
72	  decimal?: boolean;
73	}) {
74	  const t = useT();
75	  const { palette } = useTheme();
76	
77	  // Pack mode + meaningful pack_size: append "× 500 g" to the qty so the
78	  // user sees "Taken 2 of 4 × 500 g", not "Taken 2 of 4 g". Divisible
79	  // mode treats qty as the base unit directly, so just append the unit.
80	  const usePackForm = !decimal && packSize != null && packSize !== 1;
81	  const qtySuffix = usePackForm
82	    ? ` × ${packSize}${unit ? ` ${unit}` : ''}`
83	    : unit ? ` ${unit}` : '';
84	
85	  // The maximum across all lots — surfaces the real ceiling so the user
86	  // doesn't think "Taken 1 of 1" means only 1 exists when really 4 do.
87	  const grandTotal = useMemo(
88	    () => lots.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.available || 0), 0),
89	    [lots],
90	  );
91	
92	  const total = useMemo(() => lots.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.take || 0), 0), [lots]);
93	
94	  // Auto-seed allocation with FEFO when the parent passes the freshly-
95	  // loaded availability without any takes set. Idempotent — caller
96	  // controls reseeding by changing the `lots` reference.
97	  useEffect(() => {
98	    const noneAllocated = lots.every((l) => l.take === 0);
99	    if (noneAllocated && desiredQty > 0) {
100	      onChange(suggestFEFO(lots, desiredQty));
101	    }
102	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
103	  }, [lots.length]);
104	
105	  if (lots.length === 0) {
106	    return (
107	      <View>
108	        <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
109	          —
110	        </Text>
111	      </View>
112	    );
113	  }
114	
115	  return (
116	    <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
117	      <View
118	        style={{
119	          flexDirection: 'row',
120	          justifyContent: 'space-between',
121	          alignItems: 'baseline',
122	        }}
123	      >
124	        <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
125	          {t('pickFromBatch').toUpperCase()}
126	        </Text>
127	        <Text
128	          variant="bodyMedium"
129	          color={total === desiredQty ? palette.primary : palette.onSurfaceVariant}
130	        >
131	          {t('takenTotal')}: {total}{qtySuffix} / {grandTotal}{qtySuffix} {t('inStock')}
132	        </Text>
133	      </View>
134	
135	      {lots.map((lot, i) => {
136	        const isFefoSuggested = i === 0;
137	        return (
138	          <View
139	            key={`${lot.expiry ?? '__none__'}-${i}`}
140	            style={[
141	              styles.row,
142	              {
143	                backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
144	                borderColor:
145	                  isFefoSuggested && lot.take > 0
146	                    ? palette.primary
147	                    : palette.outlineVariant,
148	                borderWidth: isFefoSuggested && lot.take > 0 ? 2 : 1,
149	              },
150	            ]}
151	          >
152	            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
153	              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.sm }}>
154	                <Text variant="titleMedium" color={palette.onSurface} style={{ letterSpacing: 1 }}>
155	                  {formatForDisplay(lot.expiry)}
156	                </Text>
157	                {isFefoSuggested && (
158	                  <Text variant="labelMedium" color={palette.primary}>
159	                    ← {t('suggested')}
160	                  </Text>
161	                )}
162	              </View>
163	              <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: 2 }}>
164	                {lot.available}{qtySuffix} {t('inStock')}
165	              </Text>
166	            </View>
167	            {decimal ? (
168	              <TextInput
169	                value={lot.take === 0 ? '' : String(lot.take)}
170	                onChangeText={(txt) => {
171	                  const cleaned = txt.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '');
172	                  const n = cleaned === '' || cleaned === '.' ? 0 : Number(cleaned);
173	                  const safe = Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
174	                  const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(lot.available, safe));
175	                  onChange(lots.map((l, idx) => (idx === i ? { ...l, take: clamped } : l)));
176	                }}
177	                keyboardType="decimal-pad"
178	                placeholder="0"
179	                placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
180	                style={[
181	                  styles.decimalInput,
182	                  {
183	                    borderColor: palette.outline,
184	                    color: palette.onSurface,
185	                    backgroundColor: palette.surface,
186	                  },
187	                ]}
188	              />
189	            ) : (
190	              <CompactStepper
191	                value={lot.take}
192	                onChange={(v) => {
193	                  const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(lot.available, v));
194	                  onChange(lots.map((l, idx) => (idx === i ? { ...l, take: clamped } : l)));
195	                }}
196	                min={0}
197	                max={lot.available}
198	              />
199	            )}
200	          </View>
201	        );
202	      })}
203	    </View>
204	  );
205	}
206

210	    alignItems: 'center',
211	    padding: spacing.md,
212	    borderRadius: radius.md,
213	    gap: spacing.md,
214	  },

Clean. New picker layout:

```
PICK FROM BATCH                              ┌────────┐
1 = × 500 g                                  │ 2 / 4  │  ← pill turns
                                             └────────┘     primary when full

┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 15/08/2026  ← suggested                                │
│ 1 / 1                                          [− 1 +] │  ← "1" colored
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘     when row full

│ 10/09/2026                                             │
│ 1 / 2                                          [− 1 +] │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

- Pack/unit info shows **once** in the header (`1 = × 500 g`). No more triple repetition.
- Header pill is big and turns solid primary when `total === grandTotal`.

> DEVELOPER

can we improve the UI and UX of expiry flow, like batch expiry and single expiry and no expiry, everywhere, like in all screen where we are putting or changing expiry. what do you suggest?

> AGENT

Yes — the expiry UI has accumulated friction. Before redesigning, here's what bugs me about the current state:

**Pain points I see**

1. **Two controls for the same field per row.** Each batch has a tappable date *and* a "no expiry" checkbox below it. Which wins is visually ambiguous, and selecting "no expiry" still leaves the date pressable visible.
2. **No quick presets.** Most products have predictable shelf lives ("6 months", "1 year"). Today every batch needs a full date-picker round-trip.
3. **Inconsistent date formats across screens.** `15/08/2026` in some places, `15 Aug 2026` in others, ISO in alerts.
4. **No urgency signal.** Expiry dates render in plain text. A pack expiring next week looks identical to one expiring in 2030.
5. **Batch mode is the only mode.** Even for "one pack, one expiry" (the 90% case) you still see the batch-list scaffolding.

**Three directions to fix this. Pick one.**

---

**Option A — Smart chips, single field per batch**

Replace the date-pressable + no-expiry checkbox with **one** row of chips:

```
EXPIRY                 [ 3m ] [ 6m ] [ 1y ] [ Pick date ] [ No expiry ]
                                                                        
                          ─ or after picking ─
                       ┌─────────────────────────┐
                       │ 15 Aug 2026 · in 4 mo   │  [×]
                       └─────────────────────────┘
```

- One tap = one expiry chosen, including "no expiry".
- "Pick date" opens the picker only when needed.
- After selection, replaces with a chip showing date + relative ("in 4 months", "expired 2 weeks ago", "in 5 days") colored by urgency.

Lowest cognitive load, fewest controls. My recommendation.

---

**Option B — Default single, expand to batches**

Most receipts have one expiry. Show:

```
EXPIRY                                          [ Pick date | No expiry ]
                                                                       
─ small link below ─
+ Different expiries on different packs? Split into batches
```

Tapping "Split into batches" reveals the multi-row editor exactly as today, but the 90% case never sees it. This is structural — changes the mental model.

Could be combined with Option A on each row.

---

**Option C — Calendar-first picker with presets**

Keep one control, but make the picker itself smarter — open a sheet with the calendar plus preset rows at the top ("1 month from today", "6 months", "1 year", "no expiry"). One picker, multiple ways to set it.

Less code churn than A; more polish than today.

---

**Other improvements that apply regardless**

- Standardize on **"15 Aug 2026"** everywhere (avoids ambiguity between dd/mm and mm/dd, more readable than ISO).
- **Urgency colors** on every expiry chip/label:
  - red if expired
  - amber if ≤30 days
  - neutral otherwise (no green — green looks like "good", and "expires in 2 years" isn't an achievement)
- **Relative time hint** next to the date everywhere it appears ("in 3 months", "in 8 days", "expired 1 week ago"). One glance, no math.
- **Sort batches by date** automatically (FEFO already in lots; do the same in the editor for visual consistency).

---

Which option do you want me to implement — **A**, **B**, or **C** — and do you want the cross-cutting improvements (date format, urgency colors, relative time) layered on top?

> DEVELOPER

okayw we will keep the display format like this as standard : 15 Aug 2026
no we would not give preset of 3 month and others, let users pick exact date. 
yes , we will have the urgency colors. 
also we will have relative time hint also. 
Yes, sort batches by date automatically.
rest we do like option A.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/utils/expiry.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Expiry display helpers — single source of truth for how dates and\n * their urgency are rendered across the field-app. Every screen that\n * shows an expiry should use these so the format stays consistent\n * (\"15 Aug 2026\") and urgency colors follow one rule set.\n */\n\nexport type ExpiryUrgency = 'expired' | 'soon' | 'ok' | 'none';\n\nconst MONTHS_SHORT = [\n  'Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun',\n  'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec',\n];\n\nconst MS_PER_DAY = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n\n/** Parse an ISO YYYY-MM-DD into a local-midnight Date. Returns null if\n *  the string is missing or malformed. We construct via local-midnight\n *  so day diffs aren't off-by-one near timezone boundaries. */\nfunction parseISO(iso: string | null | undefined): Date | null {\n  if (!iso) return null;\n  const m = /^(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})$/.exec(iso);\n  if (!m) return null;\n  const [, y, mo, d] = m;\n  return new Date(Number(y), Number(mo) - 1, Number(d));\n}\n\nfunction startOfToday(): Date {\n  const now = new Date();\n  return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());\n}\n\n/** \"15 Aug 2026\" or \"No expiry\". */\nexport function formatExpiry(iso: string | null | undefined): string {\n  const d = parseISO(iso ?? null);\n  if (!d) return 'No expiry';\n  const day = d.getDate();\n  const mo = MONTHS_SHORT[d.getMonth()];\n  const yr = d.getFullYear();\n  return `${day} ${mo} ${yr}`;\n}\n\n/** Days from today to the given date. Positive = future, negative = past.\n *  Returns null when the input has no expiry. */\nexport function daysUntilExpiry(iso: string | null | undefined): number | null {\n  const d = parseISO(iso ?? null);\n  if (!d) return null;\n  const today = startOfToday();\n  return Math.round((d.getTime() - today.getTime()) / MS_PER_DAY);\n}\n\n/** Urgency bucket: 'expired' (past), 'soon' (≤30 days), 'ok' (later),\n *  'none' (no expiry). Drives color choice in the UI. */\nexport function expiryUrgency(iso: string | null | undefined): ExpiryUrgency {\n  const days = daysUntilExpiry(iso);\n  if (days === null) return 'none';\n  if (days < 0) return 'expired';\n  if (days <= 30) return 'soon';\n  return 'ok';\n}\n\n/** Human-readable relative time hint: \"in 8 days\", \"in 3 months\",\n *  \"expired 2 weeks ago\", \"today\", \"tomorrow\". null when no expiry. */\nexport function relativeExpiry(iso: string | null | undefined): string | null {\n  const days = daysUntilExpiry(iso);\n  if (days === null) return null;\n  if (days === 0) return 'expires today';\n  if (days === 1) return 'expires tomorrow';\n  if (days === -1) return 'expired yesterday';\n  if (days > 0) {\n    if (days < 14) return `in ${days} days`;\n    if (days < 60) return `in ${Math.round(days / 7)} weeks`;\n    if (days < 365 * 2) return `in ${Math.round(days / 30)} months`;\n    return `in ${Math.round(days / 365)} years`;\n  }\n  // Past\n  const abs = -days;\n  if (abs < 14) return `expired ${abs} days ago`;\n  if (abs < 60) return `expired ${Math.round(abs / 7)} weeks ago`;\n  if (abs < 365 * 2) return `expired ${Math.round(abs / 30)} months ago`;\n  return `expired ${Math.round(abs / 365)} years ago`;\n}\n\n/** Theme-color lookup table for an urgency bucket. Pass a theme palette\n *  in; this returns the foreground + background pair to use for a chip\n *  or label. Neutral 'ok' uses surface tones so non-urgent expiries don't\n *  steal attention; 'none' uses outline-only because \"no expiry\" is\n *  informational, not a status. */\nexport function urgencyColors(\n  urgency: ExpiryUrgency,\n  palette: {\n    error: string;\n    onError: string;\n    errorContainer: string;\n    onErrorContainer: string;\n    warn: string;\n    onWarn: string;\n    warnContainer: string;\n    onWarnContainer: string;\n    surfaceContainerHighest: string;\n    onSurface: string;\n    onSurfaceVariant: string;\n    outline: string;\n    outlineVariant: string;\n  },\n): { fg: string; bg: string; border: string } {\n  switch (urgency) {\n    case 'expired':\n      return {\n        fg: palette.onErrorContainer,\n        bg: palette.errorContainer,\n        border: palette.error,\n      };\n    case 'soon':\n      return {\n        fg: palette.onWarnContainer,\n        bg: palette.warnContainer,\n        border: palette.warn,\n      };\n    case 'ok':\n      return {\n        fg: palette.onSurface,\n        bg: palette.surfaceContainerHighest,\n        border: palette.outlineVariant,\n      };\n    case 'none':\n    default:\n      return {\n        fg: palette.onSurfaceVariant,\n        bg: 'transparent',\n        border: palette.outline,\n      };\n  }\n}\n\n/** Stable comparator for sorting batches by expiry — earliest first,\n *  null (no expiry) last. */\nexport function compareExpiryAsc(\n  a: string | null | undefined,\n  b: string | null | undefined,\n): number {\n  if (a == null && b == null) return 0;\n  if (a == null) return 1;\n  if (b == null) return -1;\n  return a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0;\n}\n"
}

28	 * checkbox toggles "no expiry" for that single row. Replaces the legacy

30	 * even when the guard had three groups, not ten packs.
31	 */

42	  minDate?: Date;
43	  /** Base unit string, e.g. "g". */
44	  unit?: string | null;
45	  /** Pack size — needed to render "2 × 500 g" (pack mode) vs raw unit
46	   *  (divisible). When omitted, falls back to bare qty + unit. */
47	  packSize?: number | null;
48	  /** 'pack' → integer CompactStepper. 'divisible' → decimal TextInput
49	   *  in base units (e.g. kg, ml). */
50	  dispenseMode?: 'pack' | 'divisible';
51	}) {
52	  const t = useT();
53	  const { palette } = useTheme();
54	  const [editingIdx, setEditingIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);
55	  const divisible = dispenseMode === 'divisible';
56	
57	  const total = batches.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.qty || 0), 0);
58	  // Pack mode + meaningful pack_size: "2 × 500 g" (avoids the "2 g" bug
59	  // when qty is really pack count and unit is the base unit).
60	  // Divisible mode: qty IS the base-unit total → "2.5 kg".
61	  // Otherwise: "2 g" / "2" depending on what we know.
62	  const ps = packSize ?? null;
63	  const usePackForm = !divisible && ps != null && ps !== 1;
64	  const totalDisplay = divisible
65	    ? (Math.round(total * 1000) / 1000).toString()
66	    : String(total);
67	  const totalSuffix = usePackForm
68	    ? ` × ${ps}${unit ? ` ${unit}` : ''}`
69	    : unit ? ` ${unit}` : '';
70	
71	  const update = (i: number, next: Partial<Batch>) => {
72	    onChange(batches.map((b, idx) => (idx === i ? { ...b, ...next } : b)));
73	  };

75	    haptic.tap();

84	  return (

94	          {t('batches').toUpperCase()}
95	        </Text>
96	        <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
97	          {t('totalLabel')}: {totalDisplay}{totalSuffix}
98	        </Text>
99	      </View>
100	
101	      {batches.map((b, i) => (
102	        <View
103	          key={i}

105	            styles.row,
106	            {
107	              backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
108	              borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
109	            },
110	          ]}
111	        >

113	            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.sm }}>
114	              <Text variant="labelMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ minWidth: 32 }}>
115	                #{i + 1}

121	                    onChangeText={(txt) => {
122	                      const cleaned = txt.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '');
123	                      const n = cleaned === '' || cleaned === '.' ? 0 : Number(cleaned);
124	                      update(i, { qty: Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0 });

173	              >

202	              </View>

{ ...b, ...next } : b));\n    onChange(merged);\n  };\n  const removeByOriginal = (originalIdx: number) => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    onChange(batches.filter((_, idx) => idx !== originalIdx));\n  };\n  const add = () => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    onChange([...batches, { qty: divisible ? 0 : 1, expiry: null }]);\n  };\n\n  const labelForRowNumber = (visualIdx: number) => `#${visualIdx + 1}`;\n\n  return (\n    <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>\n      <View\n        style={{\n          flexDirection: 'row',\n          justifyContent: 'space-between',\n          alignItems: 'baseline',\n        }}\n      >\n        <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n          {t('batches').toUpperCase()}\n        </Text>\n        <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n          {t('totalLabel')}: {totalDisplay}{totalSuffix}\n        </Text>\n      </View>\n\n      {sortedBatches.map(({ b, originalIdx }, visualIdx) => {\n        const urgency = expiryUrgency(b.expiry);\n        const colors = urgencyColors(urgency, palette);\n        return (\n          <View\n            key={originalIdx}\n            style={[\n              styles.row,\n              {\n                backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,\n                borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n              },\n            ]}\n          >\n            <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: spacing.md }}>\n              {/* Qty row */}\n              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.sm }}>\n                <Text variant=\"labelMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ minWidth: 32 }}>\n                  {labelForRowNumber(visualIdx)}\n                </Text>\n                {divisible ? (\n                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.xs }}>\n                    <TextInput\n                      value={b.qty === 0 ? '' : String(b.qty)}\n                      onChangeText={(txt) => {\n                        const cleaned = txt.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '');\n                        const n = cleaned === '' || cleaned === '.' ? 0 : Number(cleaned);\n                        updateByOriginal(originalIdx, { qty: Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0 });\n                      }}\n                      keyboardType=\"decimal-pad\"\n                      placeholder=\"0\"\n                      placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n                      style={[\n                        styles.decimalInput,\n                        {\n                          borderColor: palette.outline,\n                          color: palette.onSurface,\n                          backgroundColor: palette.surface,\n                        },\n                      ]}\n                    />\n                    {unit ? (\n                      <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n                        {unit}\n                      </Text>\n                    ) : null}\n                  </View>\n                ) : (\n                  <CompactStepper\n                    value={b.qty}\n                    onChange={(v) => updateByOriginal(originalIdx, { qty: v })}\n                    min={1}\n                  />\n                )}\n              </View>\n\n              {/* Expiry row — single combined control */}\n              {b.expiry == null ? (\n                <View style={styles.chipRow}>\n                  <Pressable\n                    onPress={() => {\n                      haptic.tap();\n                      setEditingIdx(originalIdx);\n                    }}\n                    style={[\n                      styles.chip,\n                      {\n                        borderColor: palette.primary,\n                        backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLow,\n                      },\n                    ]}\n                  >\n                    <CalendarDays size={16} color={palette.primary} strokeWidth={2.2} />\n                    <Text\n                      variant=\"labelLarge\"\n                      color={palette.primary}\n                      style={{ marginLeft: spacing.xs }}\n                    >\n                      Pick expiry\n                    </Text>\n                  </Pressable>\n\n                  <Pressable\n                    onPress={() => {\n                      haptic.tap();\n                      // Marks an explicit \"no expiry\" by leaving expiry as null.\n                      // (It's already null; this is here so the user feels they\n                      // made an active choice. We update timestamp via a no-op.)\n                      updateByOriginal(originalIdx, { expiry: null });\n                    }}\n                    style={[\n                      styles.chip,\n                      {\n                        borderColor: palette.outline,\n                        backgroundColor: 'transparent',\n                      },\n                    ]}\n                  >\n                    <CalendarOff size={16} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2.2} />\n                    <Text\n                      variant=\"labelLarge\"\n                      color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n                      style={{ marginLeft: spacing.xs }}\n                    >\n                      No expiry\n                    </Text>\n                  </Pressable>\n                </View>\n              ) : (\n                <Pressable\n                  onPress={() => setEditingIdx(originalIdx)}\n                  style={[\n                    styles.expiryChip,\n                    {\n                      borderColor: colors.border,\n                      backgroundColor: colors.bg,\n                    },\n                  ]}\n                >\n                  <CalendarDays size={16} color={colors.fg} strokeWidth={2.2} />\n                  <View style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm, flex: 1 }}>\n                    <Text variant=\"titleMedium\" color={colors.fg}>\n                      {formatExpiry(b.expiry)}\n                    </Text>\n                    {relativeExpiry(b.expiry) ? (\n                      <Text variant=\"labelMedium\" color={colors.fg} style={{ opacity: 0.85 }}>\n                        {relativeExpiry(b.expiry)}\n                      </Text>\n                    ) : null}\n                  </View>\n                  <Pressable\n                    onPress={(e) => {\n                      e.stopPropagation();\n                      haptic.tap();\n                      updateByOriginal(originalIdx, { expiry: null });\n                    }}\n                    hitSlop={10}\n                    style={styles.clearBtn}\n                  >\n                    <X size={16} color={colors.fg} strokeWidth={2.2} />\n                  </Pressable>\n                </Pressable>\n              )}\n            </View>\n\n            {batches.length > 1 && (\n              <IconButton Icon={X} onPress={() => removeByOriginal(originalIdx)} />\n            )}\n          </View>\n        );\n      })}\n\n      <Pressable\n        onPress={add}\n        style={[\n          styles.addBtn,\n          { borderColor: palette.outline, backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLow },\n        ]}\n      >\n        <Text variant=\"titleMedium\" color={palette.primary}>\n          {t('addBatch')}\n        </Text>\n      </Pressable>\n\n      {editingIdx != null && (\n        <DatePickerModal\n          visible={editingIdx != null}\n          value={batches[editingIdx]?.expiry ? 35	function formatForDisplay(iso: string): string {
36	  try {
37	    const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');
38	    return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
39	  } catch {
40	    return iso;
41	  }
42	}
43	
44	/** Earliest non-null ISO date out of a list of batches. */
45	function earliestExpiry(batches: Batch[]): string | null {
46	  const sorted = batches
47	    .map((b) => b.expiry)
48	    .filter((e): e is string => !!e)
49	    .sort();
50	  return sorted[0] ?? null;
51	}
52	
53	/**
54	 * verifying  — JIT fetch in flight; save disabled.
55	 * ok         — server agrees with what we opened the screen with.
56	 * absent     — server has no row for this barcode (treat as ok — local
57	 *              view is the only truth; the correction will create it).
58	 * conflict   — server's on_hand or nearest_expiry differs from local;
59	 *              show prompt before letting user save.
60	 * offline    — fetch failed because network down; save disabled.
61	 * error      — fetch failed otherwise; save disabled.
62	 */
63	type VerifyStatus = 'verifying' | 'ok' | 'absent' | 'conflict' | 'offline' | 'error';
64	
65	function isOfflineError(err: unknown): boolean {
66	  const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
67	  return (
68	    msg.includes('Network request failed') ||
69	    msg.includes('TypeError: Network') ||
70	    msg.includes('Unable to resolve host') ||
71	    msg === 'JIT timeout'
72	  );
73	}
74

> AGENT

Clean. Summary of what changed:

## New `src/utils/expiry.ts`
Single source of truth:
- `formatExpiry(iso)` → `"15 Aug 2026"` or `"No expiry"`
- `relativeExpiry(iso)` → `"in 8 days"`, `"in 3 months"`, `"expired 2 weeks ago"`, `"today"`, `"tomorrow"`
- `expiryUrgency(iso)` → `'expired' | 'soon' (≤30d) | 'ok' | 'none'`
- `urgencyColors(urgency, palette)` → `{fg, bg, border}` for chips (`expired` → error/red tones, `soon` → warn/amber tones, `ok` → neutral surface, `none` → outline-only)
- `compareExpiryAsc(a, b)` → earliest first, null last (for auto-sorting batches)

## BatchEditor — redesigned per Option A
- **Unset state**: two chips per row — `[📅 Pick expiry]` (primary outline) and `[🚫 No expiry]` (neutral outline). One tap to commit.
- **Set state**: replaced by a single urgency-colored chip (border + soft background) showing date + relative time + a `×` to clear back to unset.
  - Red-toned chip for expired
  - Amber-toned chip for ≤30 days out
  - Neutral chip for further out
- Removed the separate "no expiry" checkbox (was redundant with the date control above it).
- **Auto-sort batches by expiry date** at render time (earliest first, no-expiry last).
- The `Calendar` icon was swapped for the more distinctive `CalendarDays` / `CalendarOff` pair.

## LotPicker
- Per-lot expiry now renders as a small urgency-colored **pill** with the standard `"15 Aug 2026"` format, plus a separate `"in 4 months"` relative-time line.
- The FEFO "← suggested" tag stays as primary text next to the pill.

## EditStockScreen, OrderSessionScreen, ReceivingScreen
- Stripped the local `formatForDisplay` / `formatDate` helpers (multiple incompatible implementations).
- All expiry text now goes through `formatExpiry` → `"15 Aug 2026"` everywhere.

Type-check clean. Not committed.

<X size={16} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2.2} />\n                  </Pressable>\n                </Pressable>\n              ) : (\n                <Pressable\n                  onPress={() => setEditingIdx(originalIdx)}\n                  style={[\n                    styles.expiryChip,\n                    {\n                      borderColor: colors.border,\n                      backgroundColor: colors.bg,\n                    },\n                  ]}\n                >\n                  <CalendarDays size={16} color={colors.fg} strokeWidth={2.2} />\n                  <View style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm, flex: 1 }}>\n                    <Text variant=\"titleMedium\" color={colors.fg}>\n                      {formatExpiry(b.expiry)}\n                    </Text>\n                    {relativeExpiry(b.expiry) ? (\n                      <Text variant=\"labelMedium\" color={colors.fg} style={{ opacity: 0.85 }}>\n                        {relativeExpiry(b.expiry)}\n                      </Text>\n                    ) : null}\n                  </View>\n                  <Pressable\n                    onPress={(e) => {\n                      e.stopPropagation();\n                      haptic.tap();\n                      // Clearing a picked date drops back to unset.\n                      updateByOriginal(originalIdx, { expiry: undefined });\n                    }}\n                    hitSlop={10}\n                    style={styles.clearBtn}\n                  >\n                    <X size={16} color={colors.fg} strokeWidth={2.2} />\n                  </Pressable>\n                </Pressable>\n              )}"
}

> AGENT

Type-check clean. Now the "No expiry" chip does something visible:

| State | What you see |
|---|---|
| Fresh batch (`undefined`) | Two chips: **`📅 Pick expiry`** (primary outlined) and **`🚫 No expiry`** (neutral outlined) |
| Tap "No expiry" → (`null`) | A single chip: **`🚫 No expiry · Tap to pick a date instead · ×`** |
| Tap "Pick expiry" → date picker → (string) | A single urgency-colored chip with date + relative time + × |
| Tap × on either confirmed chip | Returns to the unset two-chip state |

At submit time, `undefined` and `null` both serialize as null in the payload — so the server contract is unchanged. Type-check clean. Not committed.

> DEVELOPER

why is the expiry date written like this in the admin dashboard and excel sheet: 2@2026-08-29, 2@2026-11-29

> AGENT

That's a compact format I introduced when we added per-expiry lot tracking, so the dashboard's `expiry_dates` cell and the Stock sheet column could fit multi-batch info into one column. The format is **`{qty}@{ISO date}`**, joined by commas:

- `2@2026-08-29` = "2 packs expiring on 2026-08-29"
- `2@2026-11-29` = "2 packs expiring on 2026-11-29"

So `2@2026-08-29, 2@2026-11-29` means "2 packs in one expiry batch (Aug 29), 2 packs in another (Nov 29)".

**Why it ended up cryptic**: it was a quick representation for the CSV/spreadsheet cell — a single string that survived round-tripping through Google Sheets without column explosion. Fine for engineers, hostile for the admin reading the dashboard.

**Where the format lives**: `office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/inventory/db.ts` → `listStockForSheet()` (~line 1880). The dashboard renders the string verbatim.

## Options to fix it

I'd suggest aligning with the rest of the app's new format (`15 Aug 2026`) and dropping the cryptic `@`:

**A. Readable text, same single-cell shape.**
```
2 × 29 Aug 2026, 2 × 29 Nov 2026
```
Or with a clearer separator:
```
2 packs · 29 Aug 2026; 2 packs · 29 Nov 2026
```

**B. Split into chip-list in the dashboard, keep CSV string for the Sheet.**
- Dashboard renders one chip per batch, each urgency-colored (matches the field-app):
  ```
  [ 2 × 29 Aug 2026  (in 2 months) ]   [ 2 × 29 Nov 2026  (in 5 months) ]
  ```
- The Google Sheet column still gets a compact string (since chips don't render in a cell), but in the readable `2 × 29 Aug 2026` shape, not `2@2026-08-29`.

**C. Just rename `@` → something nicer**, keep ISO dates.
- `2 × 2026-08-29, 2 × 2026-11-29`. Smallest change.

Want me to implement B?

1976	/**
1977	 * Drop every learned alias for a site. Useful when the alias cache has
1978	 * been poisoned by a prior run that auto-saved wrong mappings.
1979	 */
1980	export async function clearProductAliases(siteId: string): Promise<{ deleted: number }> {
1981	  const db = await getDb();
1982	  const result = await db.execute({
1983	    sql: `DELETE FROM product_aliases WHERE site_id = ?`,
1984	    args: [siteId],
1985	  });
1986	  return { deleted: Number(result.rowsAffected ?? 0) };
1987	}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrande/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/inventory/db.ts",
  "old_string": "        ) AS expiry_dates\n      FROM inventory_stock s\n      LEFT JOIN product_barcodes pb ON pb.site_id = s.site_id AND pb.barcode = s.barcode\n      LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = pb.product_id\n      WHERE s.site_id = ?\n      ORDER BY canonical_name\n    `,\n    args: [siteId, siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows as unknown as StockSheetRow[];\n}",
  "new_string": "        ) AS expiry_dates,\n        COALESCE(\n          (SELECT json_agg(json_build_object(\n                    'expiry_date', to_char(l.expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'),\n                    'qty', l.qty)\n                  ORDER BY l.expiry_date NULLS LAST)\n             FROM inventory_stock_lots l\n            WHERE l.site_id = s.site_id AND l.barcode = s.barcode AND l.qty > 0),\n          '[]'::json\n        ) AS lots\n      FROM inventory_stock s\n      LEFT JOIN product_barcodes pb ON pb.site_id = s.site_id AND pb.barcode = s.barcode\n      LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = pb.product_id\n      WHERE s.site_id = ?\n      ORDER BY canonical_name\n    `,\n    args: [siteId, siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows.map((r) => {\n    const row = r as Record<string, unknown>;\n    let lots = row.lots as Array<{ expiry_date: string | null; qty: number }> | string | null;\n    if (typeof lots === 'string') {\n      try { lots = JSON.parse(lots); } catch { lots = []; }\n    }\n    if (!Array.isArray(lots)) lots = [];\n    return {\n      ...(row as Omit<StockSheetRow, 'lots'>),\n      lots: lots.map((l) => ({\n        expiry_date: l.expiry_date ?? null,\n        qty: Number(l.qty),\n      })),\n    } as StockSheetRow;\n  });\n}"

  "new_string": "        ) AS expiry_dates,\n        COALESCE(\n          (SELECT json_agg(json_build_object(\n                    'expiry_date', to_char(l.expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'),\n                    'qty', l.qty)\n                  ORDER BY l.expiry_date NULLS LAST)\n             FROM inventory_stock_lots l\n            WHERE l.site_id = s.site_id AND l.barcode = s.barcode AND l.qty > 0),\n          '[]'::json\n        ) AS lots\n      FROM inventory_stock s\n      LEFT JOIN product_barcodes pb ON pb.site_id = s.site_id AND pb.barcode = s.barcode\n      LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = pb.product_id\n      WHERE s.site_id = ?\n      ORDER BY canonical_name\n    `,\n    args: [siteId, siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows.map((r) => {\n    const row = r as Record<string, unknown>;\n    let lots = row.lots as Array<{ expiry_date: string | null; qty: number }> | string | null;\n    if (typeof lots === 'string') {\n      try { lots = JSON.parse(lots); } catch { lots = []; }\n    }\n    if (!Array.isArray(lots)) lots = [];\n    return {\n      ...(row as Omit<StockSheetRow, 'lots'>),\n      lots: lots.map((l) => ({\n        expiry_date: l.expiry_date ?? null,\n        qty: Number(l.qty),\n      })),\n    } as StockSheetRow;\n  });\n}"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/ops-dashboard/src/modules/inventory/expiry.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Expiry display helpers for the admin dashboard. Mirrors the field-app's\n * `src/utils/expiry.ts` so guards and admins see the same date format\n * (\"15 Aug 2026\"), relative-time wording (\"in 3 months\"), and urgency\n * buckets (red / amber / neutral).\n */\n\nexport type ExpiryUrgency = 'expired' | 'soon' | 'ok' | 'none';\n\nconst MONTHS_SHORT = [\n  'Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun',\n  'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec',\n];\n\nconst MS_PER_DAY = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n\nfunction parseISO(iso: string | null | undefined): Date | null {\n  if (!iso) return null;\n  const m = /^(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})$/.exec(iso);\n  if (!m) return null;\n  const [, y, mo, d] = m;\n  return new Date(Number(y), Number(mo) - 1, Number(d));\n}\n\nfunction startOfToday(): Date {\n  const now = new Date();\n  return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());\n}\n\nexport function formatExpiry(iso: string | null | undefined): string {\n  const d = parseISO(iso ?? null);\n  if (!d) return 'No expiry';\n  return `${d.getDate()} ${MONTHS_SHORT[d.getMonth()]} ${d.getFullYear()}`;\n}\n\nexport function daysUntilExpiry(iso: string | null | undefined): number | null {\n  const d = parseISO(iso ?? null);\n  if (!d) return null;\n  return Math.round((d.getTime() - startOfToday().getTime()) / MS_PER_DAY);\n}\n\nexport function expiryUrgency(iso: string | null | undefined): ExpiryUrgency {\n  const days = daysUntilExpiry(iso);\n  if (days === null) return 'none';\n  if (days < 0) return 'expired';\n  if (days <= 30) return 'soon';\n  return 'ok';\n}\n\nexport function relativeExpiry(iso: string | null | undefined): string | null {\n  const days = daysUntilExpiry(iso);\n  if (days === null) return null;\n  if (days === 0) return 'expires today';\n  if (days === 1) return 'tomorrow';\n  if (days === -1) return 'yesterday';\n  if (days > 0) {\n    if (days < 14) return `in ${days} days`;\n    if (days < 60) return `in ${Math.round(days / 7)} weeks`;\n    if (days < 365 * 2) return `in ${Math.round(days / 30)} months`;\n    return `in ${Math.round(days / 365)} years`;\n  }\n  const abs = -days;\n  if (abs < 14) return `${abs} days ago`;\n  if (abs < 60) return `${Math.round(abs / 7)} weeks ago`;\n  if (abs < 365 * 2) return `${Math.round(abs / 30)} months ago`;\n  return `${Math.round(abs / 365)} years ago`;\n}\n\n/** CSS colors (fg/bg/border) for each urgency bucket. Uses simple hex\n *  rather than CSS variables because the dashboard theme tokens are\n *  light/neutral-only — no warn/error containers yet. Light-mode tuned. */\nexport function urgencyColors(urgency: ExpiryUrgency): {\n  fg: string;\n  bg: string;\n  border: string;\n} {\n  switch (urgency) {\n    case 'expired':\n      return { fg: '#7F1D1D', bg: '#FEE2E2', border: '#FCA5A5' };\n    case 'soon':\n      return { fg: '#7C2D12', bg: '#FEF3C7', border: '#FCD34D' };\n    case 'ok':\n      return { fg: '#1F2937', bg: '#F3F4F6', border: '#D1D5DB' };\n    case 'none':\n    default:\n      return { fg: '#6B7280', bg: 'transparent', border: '#D1D5DB' };\n  }\n}\n"
}

Clean across both. Summary:

**Engine (`office-ops-engine/`)**
- `listStockForSheet()` now writes the Sheet/legacy string as `"2 × 29 Aug 2026, 2 × 29 Nov 2026"` instead of `"2@2026-08-29, 2@2026-11-29"`.
- The snapshot response gains a `lots: [{expiry_date, qty}]` array (FEFO-ordered) — the dashboard's source of truth for rendering chips.

**Dashboard (`ops-dashboard/`)**
- New `modules/inventory/expiry.ts` — mirrors the field-app helpers (`formatExpiry`, `relativeExpiry`, `expiryUrgency`, `urgencyColors`).
- New `<ExpiryCell>` component in the Stock view: renders one urgency-colored pill per batch with `qty × date · in 3 months`. Falls back to the compact string for legacy rows without lot data.
- The Google Sheet export remains a single readable string ("`2 × 29 Aug 2026, 2 × 29 Nov 2026`") since spreadsheet cells can't render chips.

Type-check clean across both subrepos. Not committed.

can we improve the design and UX of the batch card, like the placement of x button, the increment decrement button, the serial number with #1 . It looks barebone, I want it to look good and reasonable and easy to understand. can you suggest some ideas?

> DEVELOPER

can we improve the design and UX of the batch card, like the placement of x button, the increment decrement button, the serial number with #1 . It looks barebone, I want it to look good and reasonable and easy to understand. can you suggest some ideas? in field-app

> AGENT

Some directions, with the trade-offs:

**Issue with the current card**
- `#1` looks like a debug label, not a UI element.
- The trash X sits center-right and isn't aligned with anything; tap area is small.
- Stepper + expiry chip + (sometimes) X are all on the same horizontal line — competing for attention.
- No visual hierarchy between "the number of packs" (the main action) and "when do they expire" (the secondary action).

**Three concrete options**

**Option 1 — Header + stacked fields**
```
┌─ Batch 1 ─────────────────────── × ─┐
│                                      │
│   HOW MANY?       [ − 2 + ]          │
│                                      │
│   EXPIRES         [📅 15 Aug 2026 · in 4 months  ×] │
└──────────────────────────────────────┘
```
- "Batch 1" as a clean inline label, top-left, in a muted label style.
- Trash × moves to the **top-right corner** (always visible, easy to find, large hit area).
- "HOW MANY?" / "EXPIRES" small uppercase labels mirror the rest of the field-app.
- Most structured, easiest to scan multiple batches stacked.

**Option 2 — Number badge + tight layout**
```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  ①  [ − 2 + ]                          × │
│     [📅 15 Aug 2026 · in 4 months · ×]   │
└────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```
- Circular numbered badge (①, ②, ③) on the left as visual anchor.
- Stepper + expiry chip stacked next to it.
- More compact; works well when you have 3-4 batches.

**Option 3 — Two-column on wider screens**
```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Batch 1                                       × │
│                                                  │
│ Qty                    Expiry                    │
│ [ − 2 + ]              [📅 15 Aug · in 4mo · ×] │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```
- Side-by-side fields with column labels.
- Reads like a form. Cleanest, but takes more vertical space.

**Cross-cutting polish (applies to whichever option you pick)**

1. **Subtle elevation**: light shadow + 1.5px border instead of just a flat border — feels like a card, not a div.
2. **Active batch gets primary-tinted border** (the one the user is currently editing).
3. **Trash button**: ghost icon button with a destructive (red) tint on tap, with a confirmation if qty > 0 (avoid accidental loss). 36px hit area minimum.
4. **Animate transitions**: when a date is picked, the chip fades in. When a batch is removed, the card collapses.
5. **Empty state**: when no qty has been set, the card looks slightly muted (lower opacity) — encourages filling it in.

**My pick: Option 1.** Clearest section labels, biggest tap targets for the × and stepper, scales well from 1 batch to 5. Option 2 is tempting for density but the small ① badge gets lost on a mid-range Android display.

Which one do you want — 1, 2, or 3?
